nīrāṭṭuviḻā aḻakai virumpip pārppavaṉ pōlat tattai nīrāṭum tuṟaikku mikavum aṇmaiyil akalāmalum aṇukāmalum pattirāpatiyiṉmēl vantu niṉṟu koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. appōtu kāṟṟum maḻaiyumākat tiṭīreṉṟu periya puyaloṉṟu vīca ārampittatu. maṟaintu ōriṭattē irunta yūki aṅkē puyaleḻuntatu kaṇṭu kaṉavil vanta celvattai naṉaviṟ peṟṟāṟ pōṉṟa makiḻcciyuṭaṉ, tamar aṟiyak kuṟippākap peru muracu oṉṟaik koṭṭiṉāṉ. yūki muracu koṭṭiyatum aṅkaṅkē oḷintirunta vīrarkaḷ ‘utayaṇaṉ vāḻka! eṉṟa āravārattuṭaṉ eḻuntaṉar. atē camayattil nakaruḷ yūkiyāl ēvappaṭṭa kaḷḷa makaḷir ūrukkut tīyiṭṭaṉar. eṅkum eḻuntatu peruntī. makkaḷukku oṉṟumē puriyavillai. kuḻantaikaḷum mutiyōrum kaṉṟu kaṟavaikaḷumāka nakaril palar tīkkiraiyāki viṭuvārkaḷē eṉṟu kavalaippaṭṭu araṟṟiṉārkaḷ, nīrāṭu tuṟaiyilirunta makkaḷ. nīrāṭutalaiyum viṭamuṭiyāmal neruppup paṟṟiyirukkum nakarukkuḷḷum pōkamuṭiyāmal irutalaik koḷḷi eṟumpu pōla makkaḷ tiṇaṟiyapōtu yūkiyiṉ kalakamum toṭaṅkiṟṟu. itaik kaṇṭu makkaḷukku oṉṟumē puriya villai. “nīrviḻā eṉa nēram terintu pakaivar paṭaiyeṭuttaṉarō? naḷakirikku mīṇṭum matam mūṇṭu viṭṭatō?” eṉṟu palar palavāṟu kūṟiṉar. nīrāṭṭu viḻāviṉ kutūkalam orē kūkkuralākavum aḻukuralākavum māṟiyatu. oṉṟum puriyātu aṅkumiṅkum oṭi ulaintatu makkaḷ kūṭṭam. nakariṉiṉṟum centī nākkukaḷ mēl nōkki eḻuntaṉa. puyalōṭu puyalāka muḻaṅkiya mēkaṅkaḷiṉ iṭimuḻakkaip pakai yāṉaikaḷiṉ muḻakkeṉṟu añci aṅkirunta yāṉaikaḷ nilaikeṭṭu ōṭiṉa. oṉṟum puriyāta mayakkattil āḻnta piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, yāṉaikaḷāl araṇ oṉṟu amaittu, ataṉ naṭuvē urimai makaḷiraiyum cuṟṟattiṉaraiyum pātukāppāka irukkac ceytāṉ. iruntum putitākap piṭittu varappeṟṟa anta yāṉaikaḷ, tam pōkkil taṟikeṭṭuc celvatait taṭukka iyalavillai. naḷakiriyum mataṅkoṇṭatu. etirē tōṉṟiya pakai vīrarkaḷai vāḷiṟku iraiyākkit tirintu koṇṭiruntaṉar, utayaṇaṉ vīrar. eṅkum accamum viyappum kalanta āravāra vētaṉai. nīrāṭṭu viḻa pōrāṭṭa viḻāvāka muṭintatu.





13. kāppatu eṉ kaṭaṉ

eṅkum kalavaram mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭa attaruṇattil, vācavatattaiyiṉ niṉaivu varappeṟṟa piraccōtaṉaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭu, ‘yātu ceyvatu’ eṉat tikaittāṉ. uṭaṉē utayaṇaṉaip paṟṟiya karuttu eḻuntatu. taṉ māṇākkiyākiya tattaiyaik kāppatu utayaṇaṉ kaṭaṉeṉac cilar ﻿ mūlam avaṉukkuc ceyti colli aṉuppiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ. piraccōtaṉaṉ taṉakku aṉuppiya ceyti kēṭṭu, kumpiṭappōṉa teyvam kuṟukkē vantateṉa makiḻntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattaiyaik kaippaṟṟat tāṉē avaṉ aṅkē niṟkiṟāṉ? takka ceytiyai maṉṉaṉum aṉuppiviṭṭāṉ. iṉic caṟṟaḷavu taṭaiyum irukka niyāyamillai. ‘vāṉaveḷi iṭintu viḻiṉum vīḻka, maṉṉar tattaiyaip paṟṟic ciṟitum kalaṅka vēṇṭā. avaḷaik kāppatu eṉ kaṭaṉ’ eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟi aṉuppi viṭṭut tattai nīrāṭum tuṟai nōkkip pattirāpatiyai viraivākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

tattai nīrāṭum tuṟaiyai aṭainta utayaṇaṉ, “iṉi ivaḷ iṅkē ciṟitu nēramum taṅkutal naṉṟaṉṟu. eṉṉuṭaṉ varuka! piṭiyēṟṟic ceṉṟu tattaiyaik kāppatu eṉ kaṭaṉeṉa āṇaiyiṭṭirukkiṟār aracar” eṉṟu avaḷ āyattārai nōkkik kūṟiṉāṉ. ataik kēṭṭa vācavatattai nāṇamum makiḻcciyum kalantu kāl nilaṅ kiḷaippak kātaluṇarvu mēliṭat talaikuṉintu niṉṟāḷ. niṟaikkum nāṇattiṟkum avaḷuḷḷē oru pūcal naṭanta vaṇṇamiruntatu. utayaṇaṉai niṉaintālō neñcam urukiyatu.

āṉāl avaṉōṭu eppaṭi yāṉaiyil ēṟip pōvateṉa nāṇam taṭuttatu. kāñcaṉamālai eṉṟa tōḻi tattaiyait taṉ kaikaḷāl tāṅkiya vaṇṇam piṭiyiṉ mītu ēṟṟa arukē aḻaittu vantaṉaḷ. tattaiyaik kaikoṭuttu yāṉai mēlē tūkkip piṭariyiṉ mītu ēṟṟik koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. oruvarai oruvar kaippaṟṟiyapōtu avarkaḷukkuḷ ētō puriya muṭiyāta iṉpa mayakkam pukuntu viḷaiyāṭiyatu. pūṅkoṭi oṉṟai mēlē tūkkuvatu pōṉṟa polivaiyum meṉmaiyaiyum utayaṇaṉ uṇarntāṉ. maṉattilē kōyilkoṇṭa kātalaṉiṉ karaṅkaḷ mēlē eṭuttaṇaippatait tattai uṇarntāḷ. ﻿piṭiyāṉai naṭantatu. utayaṇaṉ ūṟētumiṉṟi veṟṟiyuṟṟa perumitam koṇṭāṉ.

tattaikkut tuṇaiyākak kāñcaṉa mālaiyum piṭiyil ēṟikkoṇṭāḷ. appōtu vayantakaṉ taṉkaiyil kōṭapati eṉṟa yāḻuṭaṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. tattai utayaṇaṉ vaṭivaḻakai nōkki viyanta vaṇṇam iruntāḷ. appōtu tattaiyaik kaṇṭu pēca varupavaḷ pōlac cāṅkiyat tāy aṅkē vantāḷ. yūkiyiṉ aṅka aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai avaḷ pāl vivarittu, mēlē nikaḻa vēṇṭiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷai virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷa olaiyoṉṟai yūkiyiṭam aḷikkumāṟu avaḷiṭam utayaṇaṉ tantāṉ. itu mika maṟaimukamāka naṭantatu. piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi olaiyaip piṭiyiṉ mēliruntu kīḻē naḻuvaviṭṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ cāṅkiyat tāyiṭam atai eṭuttuc cērppittiāṉ. cāṅkiyat tāy yūkikkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷum cūḻcciyum aṭaṅkiya olaiyaik koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṟaintāḷ. cellumpōtu tattaiyiṭam kaṇkaḷ nīr perukka avaḷ viṭai peṟṟuk koṇṭakāṭci maṟakka muṭiyātatu. vayantakaṉum ēṟikkoṇṭapiṉ piṭiyait taṉnāṭu cellum peruvaḻi nōkki viraivāka naṭattiṉaṉ utayaṇaṉ. ituvarai oṉṟum puriyāmal aracaṉāṇaiyāl tattaiyai utayaṇaṉ kāttaṟkākap piṭiyēṟṟukiṟāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇiyirunta piraccōtaṉaṉiṉ vīrar ippōtu tikaippuṭaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar. yūkiyiṉ vīrarkaḷukkum utayaṇaṉ pōkkait taṭukka muṉvanta ucciyiṉi vīrarkaḷukkum kaikalappu ēṟpaṭṭatu. pōrākavum māṟi nikaḻntatu. piṭiyiṉ mēl tattai, kāñcaṉamālai, vayantakaṉ ivarkaḷuṭaṉ viraiviṟ ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉiṉ muṉṉē varākaṉeṉṟa piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya vīraṉ etirppaṭṭāṉ. av vīraṉ taṉmēl cantēkamuṟṟu ētēṉum ūṟu ceytalum kūṭum eṉṟeṇṇi utayaṇaṉ avaṉai nōkki, “varāka! nammaic cuṟṟip pōriṭṭuk kalakam viḷaivippavarkaḷ māṟuvēṭattiluḷḷa ﻿tiruṭarkaḷākavum irukkalām. etaṟkum nī eṉṉuṭaṉ āyuta pāṇiyākap piṭiyiṉmēl varuvatu nallatu” eṉṟāṉ. utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟai uṇmai eṉṟu nampiya varākaṉ piṭi mītēṟuvataṟkākat taṉ villaiyum ampaiyum utayaṇaṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu neruṅkiṉāṉ. villum ampum taṉ kaikku varappeṟṟa utayaṇaṉ, ‘iṉi ivaṉāṟ payamillai!’ eṉṟu ēṟavanta varākaṉukku iṭaṅkoṭāmaṟ piṭiyai vēkamākac celuttik koṇṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ. ēṟutaṟku neruṅki iṭaṟivīḻttappaṭṭa varākaṉum vīrarkaḷ cilarum utayaṇaṉ cūḻcciyaip purintukoṇṭaṉar. avantiyar vēntaṉākiya piraccōtaṉaṉ tiru muṉṉarppōy yātu kūṟuvatu eṉṟañci mayaṅkiṉar. irupaṭaikkum iṭaiyē pōr naṭantatu. koṟṟavaṉ āṇai tappiya koṭumaikku mikavum veruviya avanti vīrar oṉṟukūṭi, utayaṇaṉ cellum piṭiyaip piṉpaṟṟi ōṭiṉar. avvāṟu oṭiṉavarkaḷai vattavanāṭṭu vittaka vīrar, vāḷ koṇṭu etirttaṉar. ‘vattavaṉ vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ tattai eṉṟa ceytiyai maṉṉaṉ aṟiyiṉ eṉ ceyvāṉō?’ eṉa naṭuṅkiya vīrar, ip pōril uyirē pōyiṉum kavalai illai eṉat tuṇintu muṉ vantaṉar. muṉṉēṟpāṭṭuṭaṉ aṅkaṅkē oḷintirunta yūkiyiṉ vīrarkaḷ ainnūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭavar veḷivantu piraccōtaṉaṉ āṭkaḷōṭu pōriṭṭaṉar. irutarappiṉarukkum iṭaiyē kaṭumpōr nikaḻntatu. yūkiyiṉ paṭaiyiṉar vīciya vāḷkaḷil pakaivar ciraṅkaḷ paṉaṅkaṉik kulaiyiliruntu utirvatupōla utirntaṉa. kuruti veḷḷam āṟeṉap parantatu. appōtu yūki taṉ vāḷuṭaṉ aṅkē tōṉṟi utayaṇaṉaic cantittāṉ. piṭimītu viraivākac ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉ piṭiyai niṟutti yūkiyai varavēṟṟāṉ. utayaṇaṉukku iṟutivarai veṟṟiyai aḷikkumāṟu avaṉ ēṟik koṇṭirunta yāṉaiyākiya pattirāpatiyai vāḻttiṉāṉ yūki. naṇparkaḷ mēlē naṭakka vēṇṭiya ﻿ tiṭṭaṅkaḷai oruvarukkoruvar aṟivittuk koṇṭaṉar. cāṅkiyat tāyaip paṟṟi yūkikku virivākak kūṟi, avaḷai mēlnaṭakkum viṉaikaḷil nampip payaṉ koḷḷalām eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. avaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ yāvum aṭaṅkiya ōlai yoṉṟaiyum utayaṇaṉiṭamiruntu yūki peṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. piṉṉē turatti varum paṭai neruṅkiviṭavē yūkiyiṭam viṭaipeṟṟup piṭiyai viraivākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. yūki makkaḷ kūṭṭattil kalantu maṟaintāṉ. muṉṉar ēmāṟṟappaṭṭa varākaṉ ippōtu utayaṇaṉaip piṉtoṭarnta paṭaiyiṉar muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟāṉ. pōkiṉṟa pōkkil piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉukkuc cila ceyti kūṟic celkiṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam ituvarai nāṉ ciṟaippaṭ ṭiruntēṉ. iḻivu karutāta eṉṉait taṉ makaṭku yāḻ kaṟpikkac ceytāṉ. kaṟpittēṉ. ivaḷmēṟ kātal koḷḷa nērntatu. iṉṟu avaṉ vāyālēyē ivaḷaik kākkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ivaḷai eṉ nāṭu koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇakkak karutiṉēṉ. am maṉṉaṉukku eṉ vaṇakkattaik kūṟuka” eṉṟu ivvāṟu varākaṉukkuk kēṭkumpaṭi uraittu viṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. varākaṉukku utayaṇaṉāṟ kūṟappeṟṟa im maṟumoḻi kēṭṭut tattai caṟṟē ciṉaṅ koṇṭāḷ, kāñcaṉamālaiyaik ‘kīḻē iṟaṅkiviṭuka’ eṉṉum kuṟipput tōṉṟa nōkkiṉāḷ. ak kuṟippai aṟintu koṇṭa kāñcaṉamālai “koṭiyūcalil tōḻiyar kaikōttāṭiṉum kuravaik kūttu āṭiṉum em talaivi naṭuṅkuvaḷ. cūṟāvaḷik kāṟṟeṉac cuḻaṉṟu ōṭum piṭiyāl avaḷukku acca muṇṭākiṟatu. piṉ toṭarum eṅkaḷ paṭaiyai oru poruṭṭāka matiyāmal nīvir piṭiyai ittuṇai viraiviṟ celuttuvatu eṉ karutiyō? eṅkaḷ maṉam payappaṭukiṟatu” eṉa vaṇaṅkiya kaiyuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉaik kēṭṭāḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya cantēkam utayaṇaṉukkum purintatu.



kāñcaṉamālaiyiṉ viṉāvukku ēṟṟa viṭai kūṟit tēṟṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “aracaṉ tattaiyaik kākkumāṟu eṉṉaip paṇitta ceytiyai aṟiyātu inta vīrarkaḷ cantēkamuṟṟup pōrukku varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷai veṉṟu meṟcellutal eṉakku ariya viṉaiyaṉṟu. eṉavē nīṅkaḷ iruvarum ciṟitum añcal vēṇṭā” eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ivvāṟu kūṟiya moḻikaḷaik kāñcaṉai tattaikku āyattaic cērnta koṭṭantāṅkiya paṇippeṇkaḷ, aṭaippai makaḷir, cuvarimakaḷir, paṇiceykūṉar, tōḻiyar, ceviliyat tāyar, cañcukimutiyar mutaliyār yātu ceyvateṉṟaṟiyātu alamaral eytip pulampiṉar. nīrāṭa vanta makkaḷ kūṭṭam tuyarāṭit tuṉpattil āḻntu koṇṭiruntatu. iḵtu ivvāṟu irukka utayaṇaṉiṭam ōlai peṟṟuc ceṉṟa cāṅkiyat tāy periya paṭaip parappiṉ naṭuvē māṟu vēṭattuṭaṉ tirikiṉṟa yūkiyai avaṉ uṭal aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāṟ kaṇṭu aṟintāḷ. taṉṉai aṟintu niṟkum caṅkiyat tāyaik kūrntu nōkkiya yūki utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyirunta vivaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟi muṭivu ceyvataṟkāka arukiṉ irunta orukuyavaṉ maṉaiyil maṟaintu iruvarum ālōcittaṉar. ālōcaṉai muṭintapiṉ mīṇṭum tām cantikkum kālattai oruvarukkoruvar aṟivittuk koṇṭu pirintu ceṉṟaṉar.





14. ciṉamum cintaṉaiyum

tattaiyai utayaṇaṉ taṉ nāṭu nōkkip piṭimēl koṇṭu celvatai aṟintum ataip piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam kūṟa añciṉar, kāvalvīrar. kāvalvīrar palarum ivvāṟu añci eṉ ceyvateṉṟu aṟiyāmal tikaittiruntapōtu villum ampum utayaṇaṉiṭam paṟikoṭuttuviṭṭu avaṉiṭamiruntu maṉṉaṉukkuc ceyti kēṭṭu vanta varākaṉ paṭamāṭak kōvilil irunta aracaṉaik kāṇak kaṭuki ōṭiṉaṉ. “aracar perumāṉaik kaṇṭu cila avacarac ceytikaḷaik kūṟavēṇṭum. camayamaṟintu varuka” eṉṟu vāyiṟ kāvalaṉiṭam kūṟi yaṉuppiviṭṭu, ōṭivanta iḷaippāl mēl mūccu vāṅka vāyiṟkaṭaiyil niṉṟāṉ varākaṉ. kāvalaṉ uḷḷē ceṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ paṭamāṭak kōviluḷḷē appōtu oru nimittikaṉuṭaṉ pēcik koṇṭiruntāṉ. ‘kāṟṟum maḻaiyumāka eḻunta nilai ūrukku mutalil mikka tuyarattaiyum piṉṉar niṉakku iṉpattaiyum tarum’ eṉṟu nimittikaṉ aracaṉukkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu kuṟippākak kūṟiya nimittikaṉiṭam piracacōtaṉaṉ ikkūṟṟait taṉakku naṉṟāka viḷakki uraikkumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāṉ.

appōtu vāyiṟkāvalaṉ paraparappum avacaramum tōṉṟa vaṇaṅkiyavāṟu uḷḷē vantāṉ. “vantavaṉ mukattil tōṉṟum paraparappaik kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ ‘vanta kāriyam yātu?” eṉa avaṉiṭam viṉaviṉāṉ. vāyiṟ kāvalaṉ paṇinta kuralil, “vācavatattaikkuk kāvalvīrarāka uḷḷavarkaḷil oruvaṉākiya varākaṉ vāyalilē niṟkiṟāṉ. avaṉ taṅkaḷiṭam uṭaṉ kūṟattakka ceytikaḷ cila uḷavām” eṉṟāṉ. araciyal uṇmaikaḷai antaraṅkamāka ārāyum pōtum makaḷākiya tattaiyaip paṟṟiya ceyti eṉṟāl kēṭṭuviṭṭuttāṉ maṟuvēlai pārppāṉ piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ. tattaiyiṉiṭam avaṉukku uḷḷa aṉpu attakaiyatu. ākavē nimittikaṉōṭu uraiyāṭik koṇṭiruntālum, “varākaṉai uṭaṉē varaccol?” eṉṟukūṟik kāvalaṉai aṉuppiṉāṉ. kāvalaṉ, aracaṉukkup pallāṇṭu kūṟi vāḻttum vaṇakkamum aḷittuc ceṉṟāṉ. kāvalaṉ ceṉṟa ciṟitu nērattiṟkuḷ varākaṉ naṭukkaṅkalanta accamum paraparappum uḷḷavaṉāy uḷḷē vantāṉ. vantavaṉ aracaṉukku ēḻukōl ellai taḷḷi mariyātaiyāka niṉṟu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ mukattōṟṟattaiyum naṭukkattaiyum kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ, ivaṉ kūṟa vantirukkum ceyti tuyaram viḷaikkak kūṭiyatāy irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇi avaṉait taṉakku mikappakkattil irukōl ellaiyuḷ aḻaittāṉ. varākaṉ neruṅki vantatum, “vantatu yātu kūṟavō? ataṉai viraivil kūṟu” eṉṟu āṇai piṟantatu.

taraiyil muṭitōya maṉṉaṉai vaṇaṅki eḻunta varākaṉ, tayaṅkit tayaṅki niṉṟāṉē oḻiya vāytiṟantu naṭantataik kūṟa añciṉāṉ. “neñcil añcātu nikaḻntataik kūṟu” eṉṟu iṭimuḻakkam pōṉṟa kuralil mīṇṭum āṇaiyiṭṭāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. ippōtu varākaṉukkuc ciṟitu tuṇivu piṟantatu. aracaṉ taṉ uyirukku apayamaḷippāṉ eṉṟa nampikkaiyum tōṉṟiyatu.

nikaḻntavaṟṟai ovvoṉṟākak kūṟiṉāṉ: “utayaṇaṉ tattaiyuṭaṉ piṭiyēṟit taṉ nāṭu nōkkic celkiṟāṉ. piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟa nam paṭai māṟṟār paṭaiyāl aḻintu taṭai yuṟṟatu. yāṉ viraintu piṭiyaip piṉpaṟṟi neruṅkiṉēṉ. ‘tattaiyai eṉpāl aṭaikkalam aḷitta uṅkaḷ vēntar pirāṉukku eṉ vaṇakkattaik kūṟuka’ eṉṟu kaikūppi viṭṭuk kāṟṟeṉa viraivuṭaṉ piṭiyaic celutti maṟaintāṉ utayaṇaṉ” eṉṟu varākaṉ kūṟiyatum piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya virinta malarviḻikaḷ civantaṉa. puruvaṅkaḷ nerintaṉa. mukattil ciṉatti eḻuntu paravip paṭarvatu terintatu. utayaṇaṉ turōkam ceytuviṭṭāṉ eṉpatu avaṉukku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.

“utayaṇaṉai etirttu uṭaṉē nampaṭai puṟappaṭaṭṭum eḷḷi viḷaiyāṭukiṟatupōlum iḷamai. nalla paṇpuḷḷavaṉ eṉ nampiṉēṉ. nampikkaiyaic citaittu viṭṭāṉ vattavar kōṉ. nāṉ yāreṉa aṟiyāṉpōlum. cellaṭṭum nam paṭai. antac ciṟu maṉṉaṉaip piṭittu iḻuttu vāruṅkaḷ.” ivvāṟu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu muḻaṅkiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. ciṉam eṉpatu vēkamākap paṟṟip paravum kāṭṭut tīyaip pōṉṟatu. paṇpaṭṭa uḷḷam paṭaittavarkaḷkūṭa ataṟku mikaviraivil uṭpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ mikacciṟanta nākarikap paṇpuṭaiyavaṉāka iruntum oru noṭiyil taṉṉai iḻantu ciṉattiṟku aṭimaippaṭṭu viṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ nilaiyaiyum appōtu avaṉukku irunta ciṉattiṉāl ēṟpaṭa irukkum viḷaivukaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintukoṇṭa avaṉuṭaiya mutaṉ mantiri cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ, amaitiyākac cila aṟivuraikaḷaik kūṟiṉāṉ. “utayaṇaṉ ceytatu eṉṉavō tavaṟutāṉ! ataṟkāka avaṉaic ciṟai ceytalum kiḷaiñariṭamiruntu pirittalum periya kāriyamalla. avaikaḷai nī virumpiṉ niṉṉāl eḷitiṟ ceyya muṭiyum. oṉṟu nī cintikka vēṇṭum. ārāyntu pārttāl vācavatattaikku utayaṇaṉ evvitattilum kuṟaintavaṉ ākāṉ. kulam, kuṇam, naṭpu, nilam mutaliyavaṟṟālum avaṉ niṉṉai yotta aracaṉē. avaṉ tattaiyaik koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇantu koḷvatil tavaṟu oṉṟumillai. ūḻviṉai avvāṟu amaiyum pōtu ataṉaip paḻippatilum payaṉ illai. eṉavē ciṉakkoṇṭu paṭaiyeḻupputal ciṟanta vaḻi aṉṟu. maṉattil pakai irukkumāṉāl ippōtu atai veḷikkāṭṭal vēṇṭā. piṉpu vēṟu vaḻiyākat tattaiyuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉai mīṭka muyala vēṇṭum. uṭaṉaṭiyāka ivvāṟu pōr mēṟkoḷvatu aṟivukku ēṟṟatu aṉṟu” eṉṟu virivākavum karuttuḷḷaṭaṅkiyatumākac cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ kūṟiya āṟutal piraccōtaṉaṉ maṉattil aḻuttamākap paṭintu viṭṭatu. amaiccaṉ kūṟiya āṟutalāl maṉantēṟiya piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, taṉ kōpperuntēvikku ic ceyti maṟṟavarkaḷāl aṟivikkappaṭumuṉ pakkuvamākat tāṉē ceṉṟu aṟivikka viraintāṉ.

ivvaḷavum natikkaraiyil aracaṉukkeṉa amaikkappaṭṭirunta paṭamāṭak kōvilil naṭantaṉa. tēviyaik kāṇappōkumuṉ muracaṟaiyum vaḷḷuvaṉaik kūppiṭac ceytu maḻaippoḻutākavum paṉiporuntiyatāyum uḷḷa im mālaiyil tīrttat tuṟaiyiliruntu nakar cella vēṇṭāveṉa makkaḷ yāvarkkum muracaṟaittu aṟivikkavum, kālaiyil nakar pōkavēṇṭumeṉṟu paṇikkavum ceyyumāṟu kūṟic ceṉṟāṉ. ceṉṟavaṉ nērē tirumā tēviyiṉ ciṅkārap paḷḷiyaṟaiyuḷ nuḻaintāṉ. nīrt tuṟaiyil aracaṉ viṭutikku arukē amaikkappaṭṭirunta acciṅkāra māḷikai palavakai aṇikaḷuṅkoṇṭu pārppōrkkuk kaviṉ koṭuttu viḷaṅkiyatu. maṉṉaṉ varavaṟintu etirkoḷḷa vanta peruntēviyiṉ maṉamō, kalavaramum tiruptiyum kalantu oru nilaiyil nillāmal cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu. utayaṇaṉē kāppāṟṟuvataṉāl, taṉ makaḷ tattaikkut tītu nērātu eṉṟa tirupti iruntatu. āṉāl utayaṇaṉ tattaiyait taṉ nāṭu koṇṭu ēkuvāṉ eṉṟa niṉaivē kaṉavil kūṭat tēvikku illai.

palavita eṇṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tēvi maṉṉaṉai varavēṟṟāḷ. uṇavu muṭintapiṉ vanta ceytiyai virivākak kūṟak karutiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. aṉṟu uṇavē vēṇṭiyirukkavillai maṉṉaṉukku. iruntālum tāṉ uṇṇa maṟuttāl tirumā tēvi aiyuṟa nērum eṉpataṟkāka ētō oruvāṟu uṇavai muṭittāṉ. iruvarum paḷḷi maṇṭapattil vantu amarntaṉar. cintaṉaiyaip poṟutta varaiyil iruvarukkum oṉṟum puriyāta kalavaramoṉṟu uḷḷē kuḻampik koṇṭiruntatu. tāṉ colla vantataip pakkuvamiṉṟi eṭutta eṭuppil, ‘utayaṇaṉ tattaiyaik koṇṭu ōṭiviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāl tēvikku eṉṉa nērumō eṉṟu añciya maṉṉaṉ mikavum nayamāṉa oru vaḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟi atai avaḷukkuk kūṟa muṭivu ceytāṉ. “tattaiyai utayaṇaṉ maṇappatu paṟṟit tēviyiṉ karuttu yātu?” eṉa muṉpiṉ toṭarpillāta oru kēḷviyait tiṭīreṉa avaḷiṭam kēṭṭāṉ aracaṉ. intak kēḷviyiṉāl kōpperuntēviyiṉ tiruvuḷak kuṟippai aṟiya virumpiya piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, avaḷ maṟumoḻi kūṟāmaliruppatu kaṇṭu iṉṉum teḷivāka, “tattaikku yāḻ kaṟpitta peruntakaiyaṉ utayaṇaṉukkē avaḷai maṇam ceytu koṭuttu avaṉ nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppalām eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. nī yātu karutiyirukkiṟāy? itu niṉakku makiḻcciyait tarum ceytitāṉā?” eṉṟu mīṇṭum kēṭṭāṉ. ik kēḷvi mūlamē avaḷ viruppattai aṟiyat tiṭṭamiṭṭiruntāṉ avaṉ. tēvi itu kēṭṭu mukamalarntāḷ. avaḷ uṭaṉpāṭu aracaṉukku am malarcciyiṉālēyē pulaṉāyiṟṟu.

“yāṉaiyai matamaṭakki vanta aṉṟu, mutaṉ mutalāka avaṉaik kaṇṭapōtē, avaṉ iḷamaipolivu eṉ maṉattiṟ patintuviṭṭatu. aṉṟē avaṉ tattaikku ēṟṟa koḻunaṉeṉak karutiṉēṉ yāṉ. kuṟṟamaṟṟa aracar kuṭiyilē tōṉṟiya kōk kumaraṉ ātaliṉ, avaṉukkut tattaiyai ēṟkum takutiyilum kuṟaipāṭillai” eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāḷ kōpperuntēvi. avaḷ maṉam utayaṇaṉ tattaikkuriyavaṉāvatait taṭukkum nilaiyil illai. makiḻavē ceykiṟatu eṉṟu teḷintapiṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ aṉṟu naṭantataik kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. “tattai vattava kumaraṉoṭu avaṉ nāṭu ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷ” eṉṟu maṉṉaṉ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ tirumātēviyiṉ cevikaḷilē neruppeṉa nuḻaintaṉa. peṇṇaip pirinta peṟṟavaḷ perun tuyaraṅkoṇṭāḷ. āvi pataittu aḻutāḷ. āṟākat tuyaram avalamāka urukkoḷḷa araṟṟiṉāḷ. tattaiyiṉ pirivu avaḷai vāṭṭiyatu.

acuṇam eṉṟa vilaṅku icaiyaik kēṭṭu iṉpuṟum; paṟaiyoli kēṭṭut tuṉpuṟum. tattaiyai utayaṇaṉukku maṇa muṭikkalām eṉṟa ceyti kēṭṭiṉpuṟṟa tēvi, aṭuttu avaḷ vattava kumaraṉuṭaṉ avaṉ nāṭu ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉpatu kēṭṭu aḷaviṭa iyalāta tuyaram koṇṭāḷ. kūṟṟuvaṉiṉ koṭumpācattāl tāṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pōṉṟa payaṅkarap pirivuṇarcci tattaiyiṉ pirivuc ceyti kēṭṭapōtu avaḷukku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. peṟṟavaḷ avvāṟu tuyarkoṇṭatu peruviyappukkuriyatu allavē? āyiṉum piraccōtaṉaṉ makaḷaip pirinta maṉat tuyaram tīra avaḷait tēṟṟiṉāṉ.

“tām vēṇṭum kātaṟ koḻunaṉōṭu cellātu peṟṟōriṭattut taṅkiyirukkum makaḷir ulakil evviṭattum ilar. kaṭalil piṟanta muttu aṇivōrkku allāmal kaṭalukku orupōtum payaṉpaṭuvatu illai. tattaiyum namakku appaṭippaṭṭa urimai maṭṭum uṭaiyavaḷtāṉē? itaṟkāka nī mikavuṅ kavalutal takutiyuṭaiya ceyal aṉṟu” eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiya tēṟṟuraikaḷ tēvikku oruvāṟu āṟutal aḷittaṉa. tuyar caṟṟē vilakiyatu.

vāṉmati iḻanta mīṉiṉam pōla, vācavatattai illāta tuyaram peṟṟavaḷai maṭṭumaṉṟu, antap perunakar muḻuvataiyum paṟṟi vāṭṭiyatu. nīrāṭum tuṟaiyil kālaiyil irunta āravāram ippōtu pōṉa iṭam teriya villai. orē cūṉiya amaiti kuṭikoṇṭiruntatu. tūrattē aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka nakaril terinta pukaip paṭalaṅkaḷ mēkattōṭu kalantu koṇṭiruntaṉa. kāṟṟum kuḷirum cūḻap peytukoṇṭirunta ciṟu maḻaikūṭa - vāṉ makaḷ, tattaiyiṉ pirivuttuyar poṟātu kaṇṇir perukkuvatu pōliruntatu. mappum mantāramumākak kāṇappaṭṭa vāṉa araṅkiṉ vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa karunīlap peruveḷi eṅkum tuyar niṟaintu tōṉṟiyatu, anta nakarukku. maṉṉaṉum tirumātēviyumkūṭa oruvāṟu tattam tuyara veḷḷattai maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.

nīrāṭṭu viḻā iṉpamayamāka ārampittut tuṉpamayamāka muṭintuviṭṭatu. mutalil iṉpam. naṭuvum iṟutiyum tuṉpam.





﻿15. pakai naṭuvē payaṇam

yūkiyiṭam viṭai peṟṟukkoṇṭu, piṉ toṭarukiṉṟa varākaṉ mutaliyōr aṭaṅkiya paṭaikkut tappic ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉ vēkamāka mēlē pōka muṭintālum annilai nīṭikkavillai. viraivil vēṟōr perumpaṭai taṉṉait turattip piṉpaṟṟuvatai avaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. utayaṇaṉiṭamiruntu ceyti peṟṟuc ceṉṟa varākaṉ atai aracaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu mēlē vivarikkap peṟṟatu. avaṉ kūṟiyatu kēṭṭa piraccōtaṉaṉ, ciṉaṅkoṇṭatum cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ avaṉai aṭakkiyatum kaṇṭōm. piraccōtaṉaṉ, cālaṅkāyaṉaṉ uraiyil āṟutal peṟuvataṟkum muṉpu ceyta ciṉak kalavarattaik kaṇṭa varākaṉ, palar kuḻumiya oru perum paṭaiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭum utayaṇaṉait turattiṉāṉ. atutāṉ utayaṇaṉ ippōtu kaṇṭapaṭai. ivvāṟu tattaiyuṭaṉē oruṅkamarntu cellum anta iṉpap payaṇamum pakai cūḻnta payaṇamākiyatu. noṭikku noṭi avaṉ ceṉṟa piṭiyiṉ vēkam atikamākiyatu. atai evvaḷavu turitamākac celutta muṭiyumō avvaḷavu turitamākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tērum yāṉaiyumākat tiraṇṭa vempaṭai taṉṉai mikamika neruṅki varuvatai utayaṇaṉ uṇarntāṉ. anta nērattil camayattiṟkiṭaitta cañcīvipōla aṅkē taṉittaṉiyē maṟaintirunta yūkiyiṉ paṭaivīrar veḷippaṭṭu utayaṇaṉait turatti varum paṭaiyai etirttaṉar. utayaṇaṉ itaṟkāka yūkiyai maṉamāṟa vāḻttiṉāṉ. yūkiyiṉ āṭkaḷukku varukiṟa paṭaivīrar kaḷaippataṟkuḷ tāṉ muṉṉēṟip pōyviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu utayaṇaṉ karuttāyiruntatu. ataṉāltāṉ avaṉ yāṉaiyai vēkamāka naṭattiṉāṉ. vantapaṭai yūkiyiṉ vīrarāl etirkkappaṭṭup puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭiyatu. utayaṇaṉ piṭi uñcēṉai nakarukku appāl iraṇṭu kātaturam kaṭantu viṭṭatu. anta nērattil katiravaṉ mella malai mukaṭṭil mēṟkē maṟaintu koṇṭiruntāṉ. iruḷ toṭaṅkiyatu. pakai naṭuvē payaṇam naṭattukiṉṟa utayaṇaṉai maṟṟavarkaḷ kāṇiṉ tuṉpam nērum eṉṟu karutiyavaṉpōla viraivil iruḷ varuvataṟkākak kuṭakaṭal kuḷittuk kuṭamalai maṟaintāṉ katiravaṉ.

cuṟṟip pakaivar irupparō eṉṟa aiyam iruppiṉum iruḷum oḷiyum kalantu mayaṅkum anta mālai nēram mikavum irammiyamāka iruntatu. maruta nilaṅkaḷ cūḻnta vaḻiyiṉ aḻakil taṅkaḷaiyē maṟantu iṉpaveḷḷattil mitappatu pōṉṟa kaḷippuṇarcciyōṭu ceṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ. poykaikaḷ tōṟum nīlam, kaḻunīr, neytal mutaliya pūkkaḷ malarnta nilaimāṟi, ‘utayaṇaṉ iṉpamāka nakar ceṉṟaṭaika’ eṉṟu kaikūppi vēṇṭik koḷḷuvatupōlak kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir kūppiya karameṉak kuvintaṉa. vāṉa veḷi eṅkum paṟantu tirinta paṟavaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ palavakai olikaḷuṭaṉ tattam kūṭukaḷai aṭaintaṉa. pakaliṉ vemmai taṇintu iraviṉ meṉkuḷir eṅkum paraviyatu. mālaip poḻutu, ulakai iṉpamayakkam ceytuviṭṭu viṭai peṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. iravuk kaṉṉi nīlak karumpaṭṭāṭai pōrttu vantāḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya payaṇam toṭarntatu. pakaivarkaḷaip paṟṟiya kavalaiyum mella mella maṟaintatu. cūḻnilaiyum nēramum oṉṟu kūṭum pōtu paṟṟi niṟkum tollaikaḷai maṟantu cuṟṟiyuḷḷa aḻakai iracikka ārampittal maṉita iyalpu. utayaṇaṉum vācavatattaiyum kāñcaṉaiyum vayantakaṉum maṭṭum itaṟku viti vilakkā eṉṉa? pakai piṉpaṟṟit turattiyatu utayaṇaṉai. avaṉō taṉ payaṇattaic cūḻnilai aḻakil muḻukka muḻukka īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭē naṭattukiṟāṉ.

“nāḷai eppaṭiyum tattaiyaik kōcāmpi nakaril utayaṇaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉai āyamakaḷir etirkoṇṭaḻaikkupaṭik kōcampi ceṉṟaṭaivēṉ” eṉṟa uṟutiyai mēṟkoṇṭatupōla viraintu ceṉṟatu. pattirāpati eṉṉum anta yāṉai. ivvāṟu viraintu ōṭiya piṭiyiṉ oṭṭam, meṉmaiyai aṉṟi vēṟu uṇarvukaḷaip payiṉṟu aṟiyāta tattaikkut tuṉpam koṭuttatu. piṭi ovvōr aṭivaikkum pōtum pūviṉum melliya avaḷ uṭal kuluṅki āṭiyatu. anta āṭṭam uṭalvaliyai uṇṭākkiṟṟu. atu poṟāmal neṭṭuyirntu aṭikkaṭi vimmiṉāḷ vācavatattai. piṭiyiṉ puyalvēkam melliyal makaḷākiya tattaikkuc cōkam viḷaittatu. aṭikkaṭi neṭṭuyirtta tattaiyaik kāñcaṉamālai taḻuvik koṇṭāḷ. aṭikkaṭi piṭi kuluṅkiya vēkattil tattai, utayaṇaṉait tīṇṭa nērntatu. āṭavar uṭalait tīṇṭi yaṟiyāta kaṉṉi payirppu eytiṉāḷ. uṭal valiyil viḷainta cōkam oru puṟam. utayaṇaṉait tīṇṭum iṉpaṅ kalanta cōkam oru puṟam.

tattaiyiṉ ciṉakkuṟippai, avaḷ kūṟāmalē terintu koṇṭa kāñcaṉamālai, avaḷait tāṅkiya vaṇṇamē cila ceytikaḷai mella avaḷukkuk kūṟiṉāḷ: “utayaṇaṉ uṉṉai iṉṉum muṟaippaṭi nāṭaṟiya naṉmaṇam ceytu koḷḷavillai. āṉālum avaṉ uṉakku yāḻ kaṟpitta āciriyaṉ. āciriyaṉāka iruntatuṭaṉ uṉ maṉattaik kavarnta kātaṟ kaḷvaṉuṅkūṭa. uṉakkup payirppu ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭiyatu avaciyamillai. uṉ tantai taṉ kaippaṭa niṉṉai utayaṇaṉukku maṇam ceytu koṭukkavillaiyē eṉpatu tāṉ kuṟai. āyiṉum uṉṉai avaṉ tāṉākavē aṭaintu viṭṭāṉ, otta takutiyuḷḷa iruvar āciriyaṉum māṇākkiyumāka iruppiṉ maṇattilēyē antak kalai niṟaivuṟum eṉṟa periyōr vākku, uṅkaḷ varaiyil māṟṟaṟṟa uṇmaiyāka miḷirkiṟatu. uṉakku mayakkattai uṇṭu paṇṇum piṭiyiṉ vēkattil cōkappaṭāmal tappa oru vaḻi kūṟuvēṉ” eṉṟivvāṟu kāñcaṉai tattaikku uraittup piṭicellum vēkattil mēlum kīḻum muṉṉum piṉṉumākac cuṟṟiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ cuḻaluvataik kāṇāmal kaṇṇai mūṭik koḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. appaṭik kaṇkaḷai mūṭik koṇṭāl mayakkam ēṟpaṭātu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. pattirāpati ōṭiya vēkattil cuṟṟiyirunta maraṅkaḷ piṉṉōkkic celvaṉa pōlavum etirē eḻuntu varuvaṉa pōlavum ticaikaḷ cuḻaṟci eytiṉa. avaṟṟaik kaṇṭāl talaicuṟṟi mayakkam koḷḷa nērumākaiyāl kaṇkaḷai mūṭik koṇṭāl mayakkam teriyāteṉṟu avaḷ tōḻi kūṟiyiruntāḷ. pattirāpatiyiṉ vēkattil uṭalāṭṭaṅ kaṇṭa tattaikku neṟṟiyil muttu muttāka arumpiyirunta vērvait tuḷikaḷait tuṭaittāḷ kāñcaṉai. uṭal cōrntu kāṇappaṭṭa tattaiyai mella utayaṇaṉiṉ paranta mārpiṟ cārttiṉāḷ kāñcaṉai. karuṭap paṟavaiyiṉ ciṟakukaḷ olippataippōlap piṭi ovvoru aṭiyait taraiyil vaikkumpōtum mēlē uṭkārntiruntavarkaḷukkut tiṭum tiṭum eṉṟa oli kēṭṭu atircci ceytatu. anta oli vācavatattaiyiṉ kātukaḷil viḻātapaṭi avaḷ kātukaḷil pañcu aṭaittuk kāval koṇṭāḷ kāñcaṉa mālai. evvaḷavu pēṇiyum tattaikku anta vēkam cōkattaiyē mēṉmēlum koṭuttuk koṇṭiruntatu. atu kaṇṭa kāñcaṉai tattaiyiṭam utayaṇaṉaip paṟṟik koṇṭirukkumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tirumpi utayaṇaṉaip pārttu, vēkattaic ciṟitu taṇikkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. avaṉum atu kēṭṭu vēkattaic caṟṟukkuṟaittāṉ. vācavatattaiyiṉ tuvaṇṭa pūvuṭal paranta mārpukku iṭṭamālai pōl avaṉmēl mella cāyntatu.





16. vēkattil viḷainta cōkam

aḻakiya peruvayalkaḷilē koṭṭum paṟaiyolikkup payanta paciyakaṇkaḷai yuṭaiya erumai taṉ kaṉṟai niṉaintu maṭi niṟainta iṉiya pālai, tāmaraip poykaiyiṉ virinta ilaikaḷil, eḻiliḷa aṉṉaṅkaḷōṭu nāraik kuñcukaḷum uṇṇa eḻuntu varumāṟu corintaṉa. periya periya pāttikaḷil ōṅki vaḷarnta karumpiṉ maṭalkaḷilē kaṭṭappaṭṭa ceṟinta iṉiya tēṉ kūṭṭil iruntu oḻukum centēṉ, kīḻē pakkattil kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci tarum tāmaraic cemmalaril tīyai vaḷarppataṟkuc ceyyum ākuti pōlap poḻintatu. iṭamakaṉṟa cōlaikaḷilē veḷḷik kumpameṉak kulaitaḷḷiya pāḷaik kamukum, pacceṉap parutta mūṅkilum, paḻutta katali maraṅkaḷum, kottut kottākak kulai taḷḷiya teṉṉai marak kūṭṭamum, iṉiya tīm palāvum, tē māmaraṅkaḷum, puṉṉaiyum, cevantiyum, poṉṉeṉa iṇartta pūṅkuvai ñāḻalum, iṉṉum palapala maraṅkoṭi ceṭikaḷiṉ īṭṭamum kalanta tōṟṟam eḻumaiyum nukara ariya iṉpak kāṭciyāka viḷaṅkiṉa. pakaṟpōtilē katiravaṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷum uḷnuḻaiya iyalāta ceṟivu porunti iruṇṭavai, accōlaikaḷ. kaḻaṉikaḷ tōṟum muṟṟic cāyntirunta viḷai neṟkatirkaḷ kāṟṟōṭu acaintāṭiṉa. aṅkaṅkē uḻavar kūṭṭam ceyyum āravāramum, kaḷaṅkaḷil nellaṭippōr ceyyum kaḷic ceyalkaḷum kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. vēḷāḷa makaḷir vaḷarkkum illuṟai kiḷikaḷiṉ maḻalaic colviruntu orupuṟam; taṇṇumaiyum taṭāriyumāka muḻavuṭaṉ muḻakkum āṭavar nātaveḷḷam orupuṟam, kaḷai kaṭṭum kaṭaiciyarkaḷ varip pāṭal pāṭum icai viruntu iṉṉoru puṟam. ittakaiya maruta nilap peruvaḻiyil pattirāpati utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōraic cumantu viraivākac ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu.

avarkaḷ appōtu aruṭṭa nakarattai aṇukik koṇṭiruntaṉar. etirē periya kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷum, vāṉaḷāviya koṭi matilkaḷumākat terinta aruṭṭa nakarattiṉ puṟakkāṭci oru taṉi aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu. tāṉ celvatu akkam pakkattiluḷḷōr aṟitaṟku anta iravil ētuvākiviṭak kūṭātu eṉpataṟkāka aruṭṭa nakarattai aṇukumpōtu, pattirāpati aṇintirunta maṇiyai olikkātapaṭi ceytu nīkkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattaiyum kāñcaṉaiyum aṇintirunta kiṇkiṇic cilampukaḷiṉ kīta oliyaik kūṭat taṭukkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. iruḷ nēramākaiyāl ēṉaiyōr aiyuṟa nērum. ataṉāl yātu viḷaiyiṉum viḷaiyum eṉṟu karutiyē utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu ceytāṉ. aruṭṭa nakarattai neruṅkiyatum nakariṉ valap pakkamāka oru peruvaḻi caṟṟu otuṅkic ceṉṟatu. iṭap puṟam nakarai oṭṭi atēpōl oru vaḻi amaintiruntatu. uṭaṉ irunta vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉ nōkki, “iṭap pakkamākac ceṉṟāl aruṭṭa nakarattār nammuṭaiya celavai aṟiya nēriṭum. ataṉāl namakkut tuṉpam varalām. ākaiyāl olikaḷiṉṟi amaitiyāka valap puṟam cellum peruvaḻiyilē piṭiyaic celuttuka” eṉṟāṉ. aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka miṉukkik koṇṭirunta nakariṉ māṭap peruviḷakkukaḷ iruḷiṭaiyē terintaṉa. kaṇīr kaṇīreṉṟutēva kōṭṭaṅkaḷil iruntu maṇiyoli eḻuntu paraviya vaṇṇa miruntatu. utayaṇaṉ vayantakaṉ kūṟiyavāṟē pattirāpatiyai mella valap pakkattu vaḻiyiṟ celuttiṉāṉ. iṭai yiṭaiyē vayantakaṉ aṅkuḷḷa vaḻikaḷaip paṟṟiya virivāṉa ceytikaḷai utayaṇaṉukku viḷakkik kūṟiṉāṉ. vaḻikaḷ iraṇṭākap pirivatu maṉa orumaiyillātavar kaḷuṭaiya nilaiyai ottiruntatu. vayantakaṉ tuṇai, vaḻi kāṭṭum peruntuṇaiyāka oḷikāṭṭi amaintatu utayaṇaṉukku. neṭunēram payaṇam ceytu kaḻintapiṉ mullai nilamoṉṟu etirppaṭṭatu.

mullaik koṭikaḷ, mullainila makaḷir iṭai pōlak koṭi parappi, nakai pōla malarntu viḷaṅkiṉa. kuravu taḷavu kuruntam mutaliyaṉa mella malarntu tōṉṟiṉa. avaikaḷ malarum cevvi nōkkik kāttirunta vaṇṭukaḷ, makiḻntu tēṉ viruntuṇṇap puṟappaṭṭaṉa. tiṉaiyum cōḷamum vittiṭṭu viḷaikkum mullai nilamakkaḷ avaṟṟai varicaiyākap payiriṭṭirunta kāṭci kaviṉ ceṟintu tōṉṟiyatu. an nilattil viḷaiyattakka vēṟu cila pala tāṉiyaṅkaḷaiyum payiriṭṭiruntaṉar. mullai nila makkaḷ muyaṟcimikka paṇpiṉar eṉpatai atu viḷakkiyatu. kollaip puṟaṅkaḷilē mēyntu koṇṭirunta mullai nilattup pacukkaḷ maṭiniṟainta pālaic cēṟṟu nilattil poḻintaṉa. iralaiyum piṇaiyumākiya māṉkaḷ mucuṇṭaik koṭiyiṉ paciya taḻaiyai mēyntavaṇṇam tirintaṉa. kāyāmpūvum koṉṟaiyum kaviṉip pūttu viḷaṅkum poḻilkaḷil iṭai iṭaiyē ciṟuciṟu tōṭṭa vīṭukaḷ viḷaṅkiṉa. kuṟṟamaṟṟa vāḻkkai kōvalar vāḻkkai. pacukkaḷaik kāttu ōmpip payaṉpeṟum vāḻkkai allavā atu? attakaiya nalvāḻvu paṭaitta mullai nilattaik kaṭantu kuṟiñci nilap pakutiyai aṭaintatu piṭi.

malaiccāravai neruṅkiviṭṭataṟku aṟikuṟiyākac ‘cō’veṉṟu vīḻum aruvikaḷiṉ iṉṉicai cevikaḷukku viruntu ceytatu. aṅkumiṅkumākat tāvi ōṭum kavarimāṉkaḷiṉ kāṭciyil mikka aḻakaik kaṇṭārkaḷ. ceṇpakamum acōkamum ceṟintu vaḷarntiruntaṉa. kuṟiñciyum vēṅkaiyum kuḷirap pūttiruntaṉa. eṅkum pūmaṇam, kāṟṟil kalantu vīciṟṟu. akil cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ naṉṟāka muṟṟi veṭittiruntataṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ maṇamvēṟu tammai nukarac ceytaṉa. eṅku nōkkiṉum tīñcuṉaikaḷ. malaiyil ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil vēṭar iṭṭirunta tī antak kaṅkul yāmattil tōṉṟiya putiyatoru katiravaṉ eṉa viḷaṅkiyatu. ivvaḷavu vaḷam poruntiya kuṟiñci nilattai nūṟṟirupattaintu kāta ellaiyum kaṭantu mēṟceṉṟatu pattirāpati. etirē akaṉṟa peruṅkaraikaḷ iraṇṭiṟkum naṭuvē kalakalaveṉṟa oliyuṭaṉ narumataiyāṟu oṭikkoṇṭiruntatu. naṭuvil iṭaiveḷi yiṉṟi irumaruṅkum nīr nirampik koṇṭirunta narumatai nati, palavakai vaḷaṅkaḷum poruntiyatu. pala vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum antap piratēcattiṟku aḷikkak kūṭiyatumkūṭa. ataṉ iru karaikaḷilum aṭarnta marak kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aṇivakuttu niṟpaṉapōla amaintiruntaṉa. antak karaikaḷil eppōtum āravārattukkup pañcamirukkātu. nīrt tuṟaip paratavar makiḻcci āravārattuṭaṉ paṟavaikaḷiṉ kuralkaḷ pōṭṭi pōṭum iṭam atu. naṟumaṇam poruntiya malarc cōlaikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṭantu narumataik karaiyai aṭaintatum piṉpuṟamāka amarntirunta vayantakaṉ piṭiyiliruntu kīḻē iṟaṅkiṉāṉ. natiyaip pārtta vayantakaṉ taṇṇīr appōtu mikutiyāka oṭukiṟatu eṉpatai aṟintukoṇṭāṉ. poym maṇalāl uḷḷē āḻttiviṭātatum caṟṟu āḻam kuṟaivāṉatumāṉa oru pakutiyil iṟaṅki vayantakaṉ muṉṉē vaḻikāṭṭi nīntic ceṉṟāṉ. piṉṉē piṭiceṉṟatu. tukka mayakkattil irunta vācavatattaiyaik kīḻē naḻuvaviṭātapaṭi iṟukat taḻuvik koṇṭu piṭiyaik kavaṉamāka āṟṟil celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. kāñcaṉai piṉpuṟamākat tattaiyait tāṅkik koṇṭāḷ. piṭi mellak karaiyēṟiyatu.

narumataiyiṉ ak karaiyai aṭaintatum vayantakaṉ nīril naṉaintirunta taṉatu uṭalait tuvaṭṭikkoṇṭu piṭimītu ēṟikkoṇṭāṉ. vāṉattil viḷaṅkiya naṭcattiraṅkaḷaik kūrntu nōkkiyapiṉ appōtu iravu evvaḷavu nēramāki irukkum eṉpataik kaṇakkiṭṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. piṭiyiṉ nilaiyum atu kaṭanta āṟṟiṉ aḷavum ivai yāvum cērntu, vayantakaṉukku ōruṇmaiyaip pulappaṭuttiṉa. eppaṭiyum pattirāpati iṉṉum ōriraṇṭu kātam celvataṟkuḷ taṅkaḷaik kaiviṭṭu viṭum eṉpatutāṉ anta uṇmai. narumataiyāṟṟiṟku appāl oru periya pālaivaṉam, caṟṟut tolaivil iruntatu. antap pālainilam payaṅkarattiṟkup pēr peṟṟatu. aṅkaṅkē tōṉṟiya parukkaik kaṟkaḷiṉ karaṭumuraṭāṉa tōṟṟamum orē maṇaṟ peruveḷiyum antap pālaivaṉattiṟku vantu viṭṭōm eṉpatai vayantakaṉukku aṟivuṟuttiṉa. avaṉ maṉattiṟ cila accaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ‘cumantu vanta yāṉai viraivil cōrntuviṭum’ eṉpatait tavira, antap pālai nila vaḻiyiṉ payaṅkaramum vayantakaṉukku niṉaivu vantatu.

koṭumai, kolai, koḷḷai ivaikaḷukku antap pālai nilavaḻi iruppiṭam. aṅkē vacikkum āṟalai kaḷvar īvirakkamaṟṟa kolaiñar. vaḻi ōramāka amaintirunta pāḻaṭainta turkkai kōyilil patuṅkiyiruntu vaḻiyil varuvōr pōvōraik koḷḷaiyaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttuvatu avarkaḷ vaḻakkam. kollappaṭṭa uṭalkaḷaip parukkaik kaṟkaḷāl mūṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. veṭṭuṇṭa uṭalkaḷaik kuḻi tōṇṭip putaittuviṭuvar. anta varaṇṭupōṉa pālai nilattaip pōṉṟatutāṉ avarkaḷ uḷḷamum. kaḷḷi, muḷḷi mutaliya pālainilattu maraṅkaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē vaḷarntiruntaṉa. kuḷirp paruvattilum veppak koṭumai tāṅka muṭiyāta iṭam atu. ittakaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷ niṟaintatu. ākaiyāl poḻutu pularvataṟkuḷ nalla iruḷilēyē ataik kaṭantuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. ataik kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ pattirāpatiyai muṉṉilum viraivāka muṭukkiṉāṉ. piṭi kāreṉak kaṭitu ceṉṟatu. ataṉ acura vēkattil vayantakaṉ kaiyilirunta kōṭapati eṉṟa yāḻ kīḻē vīḻntu mūṅkiṟ putar oṉṟilē cikkik koṇṭatu. yāḻ viḻuntatai vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉiṭam kūṟik koṇṭirukkumpōtē, piṭi nūṟu viṟkiṭai tūram muṉṉāl kaṭantu ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. pirama cuntaramuṉivarāl taṉakku aḷikkappaṭṭa aṟputamāṉa teyvīka yāḻākiya kōṭapatiyai utayaṇaṉ iḻantuviṭṭāṉ.

yāḻait tavaṟaviṭṭa iṭattaik kaṭantu mikka tolaivu vantu viṭṭatai aṟinta utayaṇaṉ, “tanta teyvamtāṉē tarum! ataṟkāka mīṇṭum vaḻiyil tirumpa muṭiyātu” eṉṟuraittuviṭṭut tayaṅkāmal mēlē pattirāpatiyaic celuttiṉāṉ. kōṭapatitāṉ utayaṇaṉukkut teyva yāṉaiyaip paṇipuriyac ceytatu. naḷakiriyiṉ matattai aṭakkip piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam naṉmatippup peṟavum, tattaikku yāḻ kaṟpittuk kātal koḷḷavum ceytatu. anta arum poruḷ keṭṭuppōy viṭṭatu. āṉālum utayaṇaṉ maṉaṅ kalaṅkiṉāṉillai. ciṟu tuyarukku varuntip perun tuyarattiṟku naṭuvē akappaṭṭuk koḷḷak kūṭātallavā? eṉavē maṉantēṟi mēlē ceṉṟaṉar. vēkattil viḷainta cōkaṅkaḷai vēkattālēyē maṟakka muyaṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





17. piṭiyiṉ vīḻcci

kōṭapatiyai iḻanta tuyarattai maṟakka avarkaḷ muyaṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu vēṟōr putiya tuyaram pukuntu vāṭṭiyatu. eṇpatellai aḷavu ōṭiyapiṉ piṭi melivaṭaintu taḷarntatu. piṭiyiṉ naṭaiyil vēkam kuṟaintatu. kālkaḷum tutikkaiyum vitir vitirppat taḷḷāṭiyatu pattirāpati. ataṉ nilaiyai uyttuṇarnta utayaṇaṉ pālai nilattil eñciyuḷḷa irupatu ellait tolaivum eppaṭiyāvatu ataik koṇṭē kaṭantuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇit turitappaṭuttiṉāṉ. ippōtō, iṉṉum caṟṟu nērattilō piṭi iṟantu pōvatu uṟuti eṉpataiyum aṅkai nelliyeṉa avaṉ aṟintāṉ.

yāṉaikaḷiṉ iyalpaip pala nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilākat tērntirunta utayaṇaṉ, inta uṇmaiyai aṟintiruntatu viyappiṟkuriya ceyti alla. āṉāl, cettukkoṇṭirukkum antap piṭiyiṉ tuṇaikoṇṭē pālai nilattaiyum kaṭantu piracōtaṉaṉuṭaiya nāṭṭu ellaiyaiyum nīṅkiyatutāṉ viyappiṟku uriyatu. piṭiyaip paṟṟiyirunta nōy ‘kālakūṭam’ eṉṉum kaṭunōyākaiyāl iṉimēl atu piḻaikka ev vakaiyilum vaḻiyē illai. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya nāṭākiya ainnūṟṟellai nila aḷavaiyum kaṭantu nōyiṉ kaṭumai mikunta piṭi, ‘yāṉ iṉi uyir viṭuvēṉ. niṉṉuyirkku ētum tuṉpam nērāmal kāttuk koṇarntu viṭṭēṉ. cukamāka nī iṉi mēlē cellalām’ eṉṟu kūṟuvatupōlat taṉ uṭal veṭaveṭakkac cōrvu eyti niṉṟatu. ēṟkeṉavē piṭiyiṉ nilaiyai narumataiyāṟṟaṅ karaiyil tīrmāṉittirunta vayantakaṉ eṉ ceyvatu eṉṉum kuṟipput tōṉṟa utayaṇaṉ mukattaip pārttāṉ. “piṭi nammai viraiviṟ kaiviṭṭuviṭum pōlirukkiṟatu” eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “teppattilē nīriṭaic celvōrai atu kaviḻntu iṭaiyilēyē kaiviṭuvatupōla āyiṟṟu nam katiyum” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉai nōkki mēlum kūṟi viṭṭuk kāñcaṉaiyai viraiviṟ piṭiyiṉ piṉpuṟamāka iṟaṅkumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāṉ. vayantakaṉ muṉpē iṟaṅki viṭṭāṉ. taṉ villaiyum ampaṟāttūṇiyaiyum iṟukkik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟoru kaiyāl cōrnta nilaiyilirunta vācavatattaiyai mārpuṟat taḻuvik koṇṭē kīḻē iṟaṅkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ak kāṭci, kariya malai yoṉṟiliruntu nilattilē arttanārīcuvaraṉākac civaperumāṉ iṟaṅki varuvatupōliruntatu. appōtu umātēviyārai oru puṟattē koṇṭu tōṉṟum mukkaṭ kaṭavuḷ pōlk kāṭci koṭuttāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

ivarkaḷ ivvāṟu iṟaṅkiyatum, piṭi mellak kīḻē vīḻntatu. pakkattu vilāppuṟam taraiyilē paṭumpaṭi cāyntu vīḻiṉ, aṅkē niṉṟu koṇṭirukkum utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōrukkut tuṉpam varumeṉṟeṇṇik kālkaḷait taraiyil pāvi amarvatupōla vīḻntatu atu. utayaṇaṉukkup pakkattil cāyntu vīḻntatu, avaṉai vaṇaṅkuvatu pōlavum viṭai peṟṟuk koḷvatupōlavum iruntatu. nīṇṭa kariya perum paṭṭuppai oṉṟiliruntu pavaḻattaik koṭṭuvatupōla ataṉ tutikkaiyil iruntu irattam vaṭintu koṇṭiruntatu. piṭi vīḻnta vakaiyum ticaiyum koṇṭu, taṉakku mutalil taṉ nāṭu cellum vaḻiyil, tuṉpam vantu piṉ nīṅkumeṉṟu utayaṇaṉ aṟintāṉ. ataṉ cāvu uṟuti eṉṟu terintu koṇṭatum, atu aṇintirunta maṇi, purōcaik kayiṟu, mutukiliṭṭirunta mettai, mēṟ pōrtta paṭṭukkaliṅkam ivaikaḷai utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉ iruvarumākak kaḷaintaṉar. appaṭik kaḷaiyumpōtu, mikunta aṉpuṭaṉ ataṉ mattakattaik kaikuḷirat taṭavik koṭuttāṉ utayaṇaṉ. appōtu kīḻ vāṉattil viṭiveḷḷi mēlē eḻuntu tōṉṟik koṇṭiruntatu. viṭivataṟku iṉṉum iraṇṭu nāḻikaip poḻutu irukkum. tattaiyum kāñcaṉamālaiyum tuyiliṉṟi mikac cōrvākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. “avarkaḷ iruvaraiyum iṅkē pakkattil eṅkāvatu oriṭattil tuyilac ceytu nī kāval purika” eṉṟu vayantakaṉukku āṇai yiṭṭuviṭṭu iṟanta pattirāpati naṟkati aṭaitaṟporuṭṭuc ceyyavēṇṭiya kaṭaṉkaḷaic ceyyalāṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. pakkattilē irunta nīr niṟainta aḻakiya tāmaraip poykai yoṉṟil pōy nīrāṭiṉāṉ. nīrāṭiyapiṉ tūya uḷḷattōṭu taṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvattaik karuti nīrkkaṭaṉaiyum niyamakkiriyaikaḷaiyum ceytu muṭittāṉ. piṉṉar vayantakaṉ irukkumiṭam ceṉṟu tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷōṭu pakaṟpoḻutil maṟaintu vacippataṟku uriya or iṭattaik kāṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉuṭaṉ kalantu ālōcittāṉ. utayaṇaṉuṭaiya iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷil vayantakaṉukku muṟṟilum paṅku uṇṭu. avarkaḷiṭaiyē irunta naṭpu avvaḷaviṟku uyarntatu.

utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyataik kēṭṭa vayantakaṉ, anta iṭattiṉuṭaiya nilaiyaiyum aṅkiruntu tāṅkaḷ uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭuc cellavēṇṭiya avaciyattaiyum vivaramākak kūṟiṉāṉ. “maṉanekiḻcci eṉpatu caṟṟaḷavumillāta koṭu viṉai vāḻkkaiyai uṭaiya vēṭarkaḷ payilum iṭam itu. aṉṟiyum iraṇṭu nāṭukaḷ cantikkum ellait tāṉamākiya iṅkiruppatu tuṉpattai valitiṉ aḻaippatu pōlākum. vācavatattai varuntiṉālum tuṉpattaip poruṭpaṭuttātu mēlē naṭattalē cari” eṉṟu vayantakaṉ colli muṭittāṉ. utayaṇaṉ kāñcaṉaiyiṭam ataik kūṟi naṭantu puṟappaṭumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. vācavatattaiyiṉ nilaiyō eḻuntiruntu mēlē kālai or aṭikūṭa eṭuttu vaikka ēṟṟatāka illai. naṭaip paḻakkamē aṟiyāta pūṅkoṭi tattai. kātalaṉ maṉak karuttaṟintu naṭakka uṭaṉpaṭṭu eḻuntāḷ.

taṉatu pañciṉum melliya malaraṭikaḷ civappat tattai kāñcaṉaiyaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭu tuvaḷat tuvaḷa mella naṭantāḷ. vayantakaṉ vaḻikāṭṭa utayaṇaṉ muṉ ceṉṟāṉ. naṭakka naṭakkat tattaiyiṉ melivu atikappaṭuvatu kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉai uṭaṉaṭiyāka aṅkē taṅkuvataṟku oriṭam pārkkap paṇittāṉ. vayantakaṉ cuṟṟum muṟṟum pārtta vaṇṇam ōriṭam tēṭiṉāṉ. vācavatattai cōrntu uṭkārntuviṭṭāḷ. poḻutuviṭintu veyilum ēṟat toṭaṅkiyiruntatu.

pakkattil pāṟai pāṟaiyākak kiṭanta kal pakutiyiṉiṭaiyē oru nīrppoykai iruntatu. ataṉ karaiyai oṭṭik kōṅkilava maraṅkaḷ cila aṭarttiyāka vaḷarn tiruntaṉa. putarpōlac ceṟintu viḷaṅkiya anta ilavamarak kūṭṭam, vayantakaṉ nōkkil viḻuntatu. ‘maṟaivākat taṅkiyiruppataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam atutāṉ’ eṉṟu tīrmāṉam ceytu koṇṭavaṉāy avaṉ atai neruṅkiṉāṉ. maraṅkaḷiṉ mēlē cenniṟap paṭṭāl kūṭāram amaittatu pōlac civanta ilavampū kottuk kottākap pūttiruntatu. putarukku uṭpuṟam oru kukaipōla, iruppataṟku vacatiyāka amaintuḷḷamaiyaik kaṇṭa vayantakaṉ, aṅkē utirntirunta carukukaḷaiyum muḷ poruntiya kiḷaikaḷaiyum orupuṟamāka otukkiviṭṭup paciya ilaikaḷaik koṇṭu taittu ākkappaṭṭa virippu oṉṟait taraiyil virittāṉ. ataṟkuḷ utayaṇaṉum tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷum aṅkē vantaṉar. yāvarum anta nāḷiṉ naṇpakaṟ poḻutai maṟaivāṉa avviṭattil kaḻikka virumpiṉar. pakkattilirunta poykaiyiṉ kuḷircci aṅkē avarkaḷukkuc caṟṟē cōrvu nīkki iṉpam aḷittatu.





﻿18. neruṅkiya tuṉpam

pakaṟpoḻutu oru vaḻiyākap poykaik karaiyil anta ilavam putariṭaiyē kaḻintuviṭṭatu. aḻaṟkuḻampeṉa mēlvāṉam civappuṟa antip poḻutu mella vantatu. paṟavaikaḷellām palvēṟu olikaḷaic ceytu koṇṭē tattam kūṭukaḷai aṭaintaṉa. pakkattup poykaiyil katiravaṉukkuk kaikūppi viṭai koṭuppaṉa pōla malarkaḷ kuvintaṉa. vayantakaṉ, utayaṇaṉai nōkki mēlē eṉṉa ceyyalām eṉpatu paṟṟic cila vivaraṅkaḷaik kūṟiṉāṉ. “iruṭpōtu vantuviṭṭatāl, antak kāṭṭai aṭuttirukkum urumaṇṇuvāviṉāl āḷappaṭum cayanti nakarattukkuk kūṭap pōvataṟkillai. iruḷaip poruṭpaṭuttātu ceṉṟāl pala tuṉpaṅkaḷ nērum. tattaiyō mikavum valuviḻantu kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ. ākaiyāl ippōtu cellutal naṉṟaṉṟu. yāṉ nām naṇpaṉākiya iṭavakaṉāl āḷappaṭum puṭpaka nakaram ceṉṟu, namatu utavikku oru paṭaiyum piṟavacatikaḷum peṟṟuk kālaiyil varuvēṉ. atuvarai nī ivarkaḷōṭu iṅkēyē iru” eṉṟu vayantakaṉ kūṟiviṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ.

puṭpaka nakaram puṟappaṭat taṉṉiṭam viṭai peṟṟuk koṇṭa vayantakaṉai nōkki utayaṇaṉ cila kūṟiṉāṉ: “iṭavakaṉum yāṉumē aṟinta aṭaiyāḷac ceyti oṉṟu uṇṭu. ataik kūṟiṉāloḻiya niṉṉai avaṉ nampa māṭṭāṉ. nī nam nilaiyaiyum nikaḻnta yāvaṟṟaiyum iṭavakaṉiṭam kūṟip paṭaiyutaviyum piṟavum peṟṟu varal vēṇṭum. in nērattil nām uṟutiyāka nāṭu tirumpuvatu iṭavakaṉiṭam nī peṟṟu varum utaviyaip poṟuttē irukkiṟatu” eṉṟu ivvāṟu uraittu, vayantakaṉiṭam anta aṭaiyāḷac ceytiyaiyum kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉai vaṇaṅkiviṭṭup puṭpaka nakaram puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ. vayantakaṉ puṟappaṭumpōtu iravu nēram ārampamāki viṭṭatu. vayantakaṉai aṉuppiyapiṉ utayaṇaṉ tattaiyaiyum kāñcaṉaiyaiyum ilavam putariṉ uṭpakutiyil nimmatiyākat turaṅkumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tāṉ veḷippuṟam kāvalāka niṉṟu koṇṭāṉ. uḷḷē tuyilum tattaiyiṉ eḻiṟ kāṭciyil taṉṉuṭaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷai yellām maṟantavaṉāki iruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. nīṇṭa anta iravu muḻutum avaṉ turaṅkavē illai. valakkarattil ēntiya vāḷuṭaṉē poḻutu pularumvarai kākka vēṇṭiya kaṭamai avaṉukku iruntatu. poḻutum oruvāṟu pularntatu. ilava marattuk kiḷaiyoṉṟilirunta mayil, paccōnti yoṉṟu taṉmēl vālaic cuḻaṟṟikkoṇṭu varuvatukaṇṭu kataṟuvatupōlak kattik koṇṭiruntatu. kiḻakkē aṭivāṉam civantatu. utayaṇaṉ kaṇkaḷ tūkkattāl cuḻaṉṟaṉa. tūkkattai viṭuttup poykaiyil nīrāṭik kālaik kaṭaṉkaḷaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ avaṉ.

kālaik kaṭaṉkaḷai muṭittuviṭṭut taṉ taṉimai nilaiyaiyum taṉakku varicaiyāka nērum tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum eṇṇiyavāṟē puṭpakanakar pōṉa vayantakaṉai etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattaiyiṉ cōrnta nilai kaṇṭu avaḷait tēṟṟip pēṇumāṟu kāñcaṉaikkuk kūṟiviṭṭu, vaḻiyōramāka nakarntu caṟṟut tolaiviliruntu eṅkāvatu vayantakaṉatu paṭai varukiṟatā, eṉṟu pārkkap puṟappaṭṭa utayaṇaṉai oru tīya nimittam taṭai ceytatu. ilava marattiṉ ucciyilē amarntirunta vayavaṉ eṉṉum paṟavaiyoṉṟu palamuṟai kattiyatu. atu kural koṭuttatiliruntu viraivil evarō pakaivar paṭai taṉṉai neruṅka irukkiṟatu eṉṟaṟintāṉ utayaṇaṉ. mutaṉ mutalil tāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉāl vañcaka yāṉaiyiṉ mūlam ciṟaip piṭikkap paṭuvataṟku muṉpuṅkūṭa itē paṟavai kattiya nimittam avaṉukku niṉaivil eḻuntatu. etaṟkum muṉṉēṟpāṭāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa karuttiṉaṉāy villait tirutti atiṟ poruntiya ampuṭaṉ ilavam putariṉ muṉpu niṉṟu koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. iḵtu ivvāṟu irukkap pattirāpati muṉpu iṟantu viḻuntu kiṭanta vaḻiyōramāka vanta koḷḷaiyaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttum toḻilaiyuṭaiya vēṭar cilar cantēkamuṟṟu niṉṟaṉar. antap pakutiyil maṟaintu vāḻpavarkaḷākiya avarkaḷ vaḻiyiṟ cellum vaṇikar kūṭṭaṅkaḷaik koḷḷaiyaṭippatum kolai ceyvatumē toḻilāka uṭaiyavarkaḷ. cavarar, puḷiñar eṉṟa anta iruvakai iṉattu vēṭarkaḷum aṉṟu kālaiyil pattirāpatiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭu kaṇṭu, ataip piṉpaṟṟiyē patuṅkip patuṅki vantaṉar. aṭiccuvaṭu kaṇṭatum atu ētō oru nāṭṭup piṭiyiṉuṭaiya aṭiccuvaṭu eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut terintu viṭṭatu. atil ceṉṟōr celvamikkavarāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum aṉumāṉittaṉar. cuvaṭu paṟṟi vanta avarkaḷ pattirāpati vīḻntu kiṭanta iṭattiṟkē vantu cērntaṉar. aṅkē āṭavar peṇṭir kāl aṭikaḷiṉ aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇṭa avarkaḷ cantēkam uṟutip paṭṭatu. aṅṅaṉam cantēkan tōṉṟiṉālum yāṉaiyil vantavarkaḷ itaṟkuḷ veku tolaivu ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum. ākaiyāl avarkaḷiṭam koḷḷai aṭippataṟku iyalāmaṟ pōyiṟṟē eṉṟu vēṭar varunti uraittaṉar. avarkaḷ kūṟṟai maṟantu mutiya vēṭaṉākiya nimittikaṉ oruvaṉ, paṟavai oṉṟiṉ oli nimittattaik kēṭṭu “nīṅkaḷ niṉaippatu tavaṟu. ip piṭiyil vantōr iṅkētāṉ pakkattē oriṭattilē taṅkiyirukka vēṇṭuma. nām viraintu ceṉṟāl avarkaḷaik kāṇalām. perum poruḷ koḷḷaiyākak kiṭṭum. āṉāl vīram mikka āṇ makaṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ kaṭiṉamākap pōriṭṭē ap poruḷai nām aṭaimuṭiyum” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. avvāṟu kūṟi muṭikkavum neñcil īvirakkamaṟṟa anta vañcakar kūṭṭam oṉṟukūṭi eḻuntuviṭṭatu. piṭivīḻnta iṭattiliruntu cellum āṭavar peṇṭir aṭiccuvaṭukaḷai iṭai viṭāmal piṉpaṟṟiya vēṭar ilavam putarai neruṅki viṭṭaṉar. aṅkē cuṟṟit cuṟṟit tēṭiṉar. appōtu poykaikku etirē ilavam putariṉ vāyilil niṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉaic cilar pārttu viṭṭaṉar. “atō atō, akappaṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ” eṉṟa kūkkuraluṭaṉ ilavam putaraic cūḻntukoṇṭu tākka ārampittaṉar vēṭar. utayaṇaṉ taṉṉai etirttuc cūḻum pakaivarai vil muṉaiyil ampu maḻai poḻintu viraṭṭa ārampikkummuṉ putarukkuḷ orumuṟai tirumpip pārttāṉ. oṉṟum puriyāta kalavarattuṭaṉē kaṇkaḷil accam oḷira naṭukkattōṭu veḷiyē nōkkik koṇṭirunta tattaiyaik kākkumpaṭi, uḷḷē uṭaṉirunta kāñcaṉaikkuk kūṟiviṭṭu villai vaḷaittāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

etirtta vēṭar, itu kaṇṭu tikaittu niṉṟaṉar. tikaippu orupuṟam iruntālum eḷitākak kaiyil cikkiya koḷḷaiyai viṭṭuviṭa avarkaḷ virumpavum illai. taṉiyoruvaṉāka niṉṟa utayaṇaṉai neruṅki etirttu veṟṟi koḷḷa muyaṉṟārkaḷ. “yāṉai iṟantu pōṉatum iṅkiruntu tappi nāṭu ceṉṟuviṭalām eṉṟu karutiṉāy pōlum! nī eppaṭip pōyviṭa muṭiyum? uṉ uyirai uṇṇāmal uṉṉai viṭṭuviṭa māṭṭōm. mariyātaiyāka nī yār eṉpataic colliviṭu” eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē avaṉai vāṭṭa ārampittatu vēṭṭuvar kūṭṭam.

avarkaḷ ceyta ventuyarkaḷukkum kēṭṭa kēḷvikaḷukkum utayaṇaṉ vāy tiṟantu patil collavē illai; avaṉ kaiyilirunta villē viṭai kūṟik koṇṭiruntatu. anta vīra villiliruntu noṭikku noṭi paṟantu koṇṭirunta ampukaḷ vēṭaril palarai viṇṇukku aṉuppivaittaṉa. camayamum cūḻnilaiyum etirpārāta tuṉpaṅkaḷait tarumpōtu, etirpārāta tuṇivaiyum maṉitaṉukkuk koṭuttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kolaik koṭumaikaḷukkut taṉnikaraṟṟa kāṭṭu vēṭarkaḷai oṟṟai maṉitaṉāka niṉṟu etirkkum āṟṟalum utayaṇaṉukku appaṭittāṉ ēṟpaṭṭatu. cuṟṟi vaḷaikkum palarkku iṭaiyil utayaṇaṉ oruvaṉē palarāka niṉṟu, palarōṭum pōriṭṭa vintaiyai niṉaikka niṉaikka etirikaḷukkē viyappūṭṭiyatu. taṅkaḷ cāmarttiyattait tavirap piṟar tiṟamaiyai viyappatai vāḻkkaiyil iḻinta paṇpākak karutupavar ak kāṭṭu vēṭar. āṉāl, utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭa piṉṉar, antak koḷkaiyait tāṅkaḷākavē avarkaḷ māṟṟik koḷḷa nērntatu. ivaṉ veṟum maṉitaṉtāṉā? allatu kūṟṟuvaṉiṉ vēṟṟuruvamō?’ eṉṟu aiyuṟavu koṇṭaṉar. villaiyum ampaiyum avaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum muṟaikaḷil putiya putiya nuṇukkaṅkaḷ avarkaḷukkup pulappaṭṭaṉa. oru noṭiyil cūḻntuḷḷa palarukkum atircci uṇṭākum vaṇṇam kaṇkaḷ cuṟṟic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu paṟakkum paṭiyāka avaikaḷai utayaṇaṉ eyta muṟaiyai viyavāta vēṭar illai. uḷḷē tattai mutaṉ mutalāka utayaṇaṉiṉ pōrttiṟattaik kaṇṭu kātal urimai kalanta makiḻcciyōṭu viyantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. pōriṉ avaciyam taṉ poruṭṭeṉṟa tukkamum avaḷukku iruntatu.

vēṭarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa ēmāṟṟattaik kōpamāka māṟṟikkoṇṭu, puḷnimittam kūṟiya mutiya vēṭaṉmēl ataic celuttiṉārkaḷ. pakaivaṉiṭattil arumperum tiṟaṉaik kāṇkiṉṟa ovvoruvaṉum atai atika nēram viyakka muṭiyātu. caṟṟu nēram taṉṉai maṟanta nilaiyil tōṉṟum anta viyappuṇarcci vekuviraivil mikap periya acūyaiyōṭukūṭiya poṟāmaiyāka uruppeṟṟu viṭum. itu ulaka iyaṟkai maṉita cupāvamuṅ kūṭa inta iyaṟkaikku ak kāṭṭu vēṭarkaḷ viti vilakkā eṉṉa? illaiyē! mika viraivil taṅkaḷai uṇarntu camāḷittukkoṇṭa vēṭarkaḷ puḷnimittam colli yavaṉai vaitukoṇṭē tākkutalai valuppaṭuttuvataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka utayaṇaṉai mika aṇmaiyil neruṅki vaḷaittukkoḷḷa ārampittaṉar.





﻿19. vēṭar kēṭukaḷ

maṟṟavarkaḷ taṉṉait tiṭṭuvatiliruntu tappa oru vaḻi kaṇṭa nimittikaṉ, ataik kūṟiṉāṉ. mika nerukkamāka vaḷaittukkoṇṭu tākkiyataṉāl, ilavam putarukkuḷḷē maṟaintu irunta tattaiyum kāñcaṉaiyum vēṭarkaḷ kaṇkaḷukkut terintuviṭṭaṉar. appaṭit terintu koṇṭavarkaḷil anta mutiya nimittikaṉum oruvaṉ. “putarukkut tīmūṭṭi viṭṭāl ivaṉ pōrai niṟutti viṭuvāṉ” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭik kūṟiṉāṉ anta mutiya nimittikaṉ. taṉṉai vaitavarkaḷiṭamiruntu tappa inta upāyamtāṉ avaṉukku vāyttatu. anta muṭivai yāvarum ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.

putarukkut tī vaikkumāṟu kūṟiya nimittikaṉ kūṟṟai, vēṭar yāvarum varavēṟṟaṉar. āyiṉum nimittikaṉ ataṉai viḷaṅkak kūṟukiṉṟavarai avarkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭārkaḷillai. ēṟkeṉavē utayaṇaṉ taṅkaḷai ampukaḷāl vāṭṭiyataṉai niṉaikka niṉaikka anta nimittikaṉmēl avarkaḷukkuc caṟṟē veṟuppuṇarcci tōṉṟiyiruntatu. nimittikaṉ taṉ kūṟṟai vivarikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. “ilavam putaraic cuṟṟit tī mūṭṭiviṭṭāl, uḷḷiruppavarkaḷ evvāṟēṉum veḷippaṭṭuttāṉ ākavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuṅkāl nām avarkaḷaic cūḻntu koṇṭu tuṉpuṟutta muṭiyum” eṉṟu kūṟiya piṉpē, nimittikaṉ collil avarkaḷukku nampikkai piṟantatu. kaiyilirunta araṇik kōlkaḷaik kaṭaintu tī mūṭṭa muṟpaṭṭaṉar kāṭṭu vēṭar. kāyntu pañcu pōlirunta pūḷaip pūkkaḷai iṭaimaṭuttu, araṇik kōlkaḷai uracip paḻakkamuḷḷa avarkaḷ tī uṇṭākka veku nēramāka villai. “eṉ puḷ nimittam oru pōtum poyyākātu” eṉṟu ciṟitu tuṇivuṭaṉ nimittikaṉ ippōtu vāyviṭṭuc coṉṉāṉ. mūṭṭiya tīyaip putaraic cuṟṟilum iṭṭaṉar vēṭar. ‘taṅkaḷai vēṇṭic caraṇaṭaiya vēṇṭum; allatu vaitavaṇṇam uyirviṭa vēṇṭum. ivviraṇṭoḻiyap putariluḷḷōr vēṟetuvum ceyvataṟkiyalātu’ eṉṟu niṉaittavāṟē ciṅkattai vaḷaikkum ciṟu narik kūṭṭam pōlap putarai vaḷaittukkoṇṭu vēṭar tuṉpuṟuttalāyiṉar. tīp pukai cūḻntu putariṉuḷḷē mūccuviṭavum iyalātu pōyiṟṟu. taḻaittuk koḻittup pūttu viḷaṅkiya pūmputar pukaippaṭalaṅkaḷil mella mella maṟaintu koṇṭiruntatu. uḷḷē tattai kāṭṭut tīyiṉāl eḻunta pukaiyil akappaṭṭukkoṇṭu tikkumukkāṭum peṇ māṉaip pōla varuntiṉāḷ. utayaṇaṉiṉ taṉimai avaḷ uḷḷattaic cuṭṭatu. taṉporuṭṭu avaṉ paṭum tuyarkaḷiṉ mikutiyai avaḷāl niṉaikkavum muṭiyavillai. uḷḷē tuyaralaikaḷ poṅki mōtikkoṇṭiruntaṉa. avaḷ peṇmai avvaḷavu tuṉpa mikutiyait tāṅkum aṉupavattaikkūṭap peṟṟirukkavillai. tattaiyiṉ nilaiyai avaḷ kūṟāmalē uṇarntukoṇṭa utayaṇaṉ, avaḷarukil vantu aviḻntukiṭanta kūntalai mellak kōtiyavāṟu tiruttiṉāṉ. avaḷ tuyar taṇiyac cila kūṟik kāñcaṉa mālaikku oru poṟuppai aḷittāṉ. “yāṉ veḷip puṟañ ceṉṟu pōrceytu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭu varukiṟēṉ. ataṟkuḷ nīṅkaḷ iruvarum tīp paṟṟāta oru puṟamāka iṅkiruntu veḷiyēṟic ceṉṟu eṅkēṉum oriṭattil oḷintiruṅkaḷ. ivarkaḷai veṟṟi koṇṭa piṟaku yāṉum nīṅkaḷirukkumiṭam vantu cērntu koḷvēṉ” eṉṟu uraittut tattaiyaik kāñcaṉamālaiyiṭam aṭaikkalam pōla aḷittāṉ. āṉāl inta āṇaiyaik kāñcaṉaikku iṭṭuviṭṭu iruvariṭamum viṭai peṟṟuk koṇṭu veḷippōnta utayaṇaṉ, itaṟku avaciyamē illātu pōyiṉatai uṇarntāṉ. avaṉ villum kaiyumāka veḷivanta vēkattaik kaṇṇuṟṟa vēṭar iṉam, yāḷiyaik kaṇṭa yāṉai iṉampōlac citaṟi ōṭiviṭṭatu. eṉavē, utayaṇaṉ taṉ tiṭṭattai māṟṟik koṇṭāṉ. viraivāka uḷḷē ceṉṟu tīp paṟṟāta oru ciṟu muṭukkiṉ vaḻiyē puṟappaṭṭuk koṇṭirunta tattaiyaiyum kāñcaṉaiyaiyum tāṉē aḻaittukkoṇṭu putariṉ vāyiṟ puṟamākavē veḷiyēṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷuṭaṉ avaṉ putarukku veḷiyē ciṟitu tolaivutāṉ naṭantiruppāṉ. tiṭumeṉṟu muṉṉēṟpāṭṭuṭaṉ patuṅkiyiruntu tantiramākac cūḻvatupōl vantu vaḷaittatu vēṭar paṭai. appōtu tāṉ utayaṇaṉukku avarkaḷ cūḻcci naṉku purintatu. putariliruntu taṉṉai veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkākavē avarkaḷ añciyatupōla naṭittu vilakiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉ uṇarntāṉ. muṉṉum piṉṉum pakkamum eṅkumē neruṅki vaḷaittirunta paṭaiyaiyum taṉ villaiyum māṟimāṟip pārttāṉ avaṉ. utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu pārtta kuṟippaip purintukoṇṭa vēṭarkaḷil oruvaṉ mikka matiyuḷḷavaṉ. uṭaṉē taṉ villil ampu toṭuttuc cariyākak kuṟi vaittu utayaṇaṉ villiṉ nāṇai aṟuttu iraṇṭākat tuṇṭittuviṭṭāṉ. kalaivallunar palar tam kaittiṟam viḷaṅkac camaitta anta valiya vil, nāṇ aṟuntu toṅkiyatu. camayamaṟintu ceyta anta vēṭaṉiṉ ceyal utayaṇaṉait tikaikkac ceytatu. mēlē eṉṉa ceyvateṉṟu puriyātu tikaippuṭaṉ niṉṟukoṇṭiruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. valaiyil vīḻntu kaṭṭuṇṭa ciṅkampōla iruntatu avaṉ nilai. pāratap pōril apimaṉyuvaip pōlat taṉiyāka niṉṟu, taṉtuyar niṉaintiruntāṉ avaṉ.

tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarcciyāka neruṅki varumpōtu āṇmaiyāḷaṉukku or acātāraṇamāṉatuṇivum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. cuṟṟi niṟpavarkaḷō īvirakkamaṟṟa pakaivarkaḷ. kaiyil iruppatō nāṇ aṟuntapōṉa veṟum vil taṇṭu. accamum viyappum tōṉṟa iṉam puriyāta tuyarattuṭaṉ tattaiyum kāñcaṉaiyum aṇittē niṉṟaṉar. ‘kaiyil paṭaiyētum aṟṟavaṉ’ eṉṟa pōr aṟamum karutātu kaṇaikaḷait toṭuttavaṇṇamiruntaṉar vēṭar. veṟṟu villoṉṟē tuṇaiyāka, avarkaḷ kaṇai māriyait taṉṉiliruntu ciṟitu nēram vilakkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. avaṉuṭaiya inta nilaiyaik kaṇṭa tattai, taṉ maṉattil tuyarat tuṭippuṭaṉ uḷḷaṅkavarnta kaḷvaṉ uṭalait tuḷaikkumō eṉṟu añcumpaṭi vēṭar ampukaḷāl tākkukiṉṟa nilaiyaip poṟuttuk koḷḷa muṭiyāmal tavittāḷ. ētō niṉaivu vantavaḷpōlat taṉ muṉ kaikaḷaiyum kaḻuttaiyum pārttāḷ. avaḷ mukattil nampikkai ciṟitu malarntatu. tāṉ aṇintirunta nakaikaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaḻaṟṟiṉāḷ. kāñcaṉaiyai arukilaḻaittu avaṟṟai utayaṇaṉ mūlam vēṭarkaṭku aḷittu avarkaḷuṭaiya pōrai niṟuttumāṟu vēṇṭikkoḷḷac coṉṉāḷ. kāñcaṉai caṟṟut tayaṅkiyapiṉ maṟumoḻi kūṟāmal avaṟṟai vāṅkicceṉṟu utayaṇaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttāḷ.

kāñcaṉai vilai matippumikka aṇikalaṉkaḷai utayaṇaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuppataiyum avaṉ oṉṟum puriyātu tikaittut tattaiyirunta pakkam tirumpi nōkkuvataiyum kaṇṭa vēṭar, aṇikaḷukku ācaippaṭṭup pōraic ciṟitu taḷarttiṉar. tattaiyiṉ naṭukkamum tuyara maṉanilaiyum utayaṇaṉukku mika viraiviṟ pulappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. noṭikku noṭi tuṉpam mikuntu nerukkumpōtu, niṉaivukku aḷavu kaṭanta nuṇmaiyum vēkamum kūrmaiyum eṅkiruntō kiṭaittu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. utayaṇaṉ niṉaivil ciṟiyatōr cūḻcci mika viraivil uruvāki viṭṭatu. accūḻcciyiṉ tiṭṭappaṭi naṭakka avaṉ tayārāyiṉaṉ. tāṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉpatai uraiyāmal vēṭarkaḷai nōkkik kūṟalāṉāṉ. “kāṭṭu muḻaikaḷiluṟaiyum valiya tōḷaiyuṭaiya vēṭarkaḷē, caṟṟu arukē vantu yāṉ kūṟap pōvataik kēḷuṅkaḷ. pala periya aṇikalap poruḷkaḷai muyaṟciyāl īṭṭip piṭimītu koṇṭu, iv vaḻiyē vanta yāṅkaḷ vāṇikarkaḷ. varumpōtu ivviṭattiṟkuc ciṟitu tolaivil eṅkaḷ piṭi nōyāl vīḻntu iṟantu viṭṭatu. piṉṉar yāṅkaḷ mikavum maṉaṅkavaṉṟu vaḻiyiliruntu ciṟitu tolaivu ceṉṟu īṭṭivanta perum poruḷkaḷaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum ōriṭattil putaittu vaittirukkiṟōm. nīṅkaḷ pōrai niṟuttuvīrkaḷāyiṉ poruḷaip putaitta iṭattai uṅkaṭkuk kāṭṭukiṉṟōm. avaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē aṭaiyalām” eṉṟu kūṟi muṭittāṉ.

utayaṇaṉ kūṟṟaic ceviyuṟṟa vēṭar pōrai niṟuttiṉar. cuṟṟi vaḷaittirunta vēṭarkaḷai vilakkiviṭṭu avarkaḷ talaivaṉ muṉvantāṉ. utayaṇaṉai neruṅkiya vēṭar talaivaṉ, “nī yār eṉpatai emakku viḷakkamākak kūṟavēṇṭum” eṉṟu miraṭṭiṉāṉ. inta viṉāvaik kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ oru kaṇam tikaippu aṭaintāṉ. āṉāl uṟutiyākak kaṭaippiṭittāl oḻiyat taṉ cūḻcci uṭaṉē veṟṟi peṟāteṉpatai uṇarntu, “yām utayaṇaṉuṭaiya vāṇikar. perum poruḷuṭaṉ piṭiyil vantōm. iṭaiyil piṭi vīḻntuviṭṭatu. poruḷai vaḻikku appāl oru poḻilil putaittu vaittōm” eṉṟu muṉ coṉṉataiyē maṟupaṭiyum poy kalantu viḷakkamāka uraittāṉ. taṉatu inta viṭaiyil vēṭar talaivaṉukku makiḻcci ēṟpaṭṭatai avaṉ mukak kuṟippiliruntu utayaṇaṉ aṟintu koṇṭāṉ. tattai kaḻaṟṟiyaḷitta nakaikaḷaiyum avarkaḷukku aḷittāṉillai utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ tuṇaikku vantu cērumvarai taṉakkum tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷukkum vēṭarkaḷāl peruntuṉpam nērātavāṟu pēcciṉālēyē taṭuttuk koḷḷavē utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu oru muḻup poyyaic colla nērntatu. atu karuti utayaṇaṉ ceyta ic cūḻcci takka payaṉai aḷittatu. ‘avarkaḷ vattava nāṭṭu maṉṉaṉ utayaṇaṉiṉ vaṇikarkaḷ’ eṉṟa kūṟṟaik kēṭṭuc caṟṟu mariyātai koṇṭu, vēṭarkaḷ tuṉpuṟuttuvatai muṟṟilum nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum putaittu vaittuḷḷa poruṭkuvaiyai evvāṟēṉum paṟittuk koḷḷavē virumpiṉar. paṟittuk koḷḷa muṭiyum eṉṟa nampikkaiyil ciṟitu makiḻcciyum aṭaintaṉar. vēṭar talaivaṉ, utayaṇaṉai neruṅki avaṉ mēlāṭaiyālēyē avaṉuṭaiya iru kaikaḷaiyum piṇittup “perum poruḷaip putaitta iṭattaik kāṭṭuka” eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. taṅkaḷiṭam utayaṇaṉ caraṇaṭaintu viṭṭamaikku aṟikuṟiyākavē mēlāṭaiyāl utayaṇaṉaip piṇittāṉ eyiṉar talaivaṉ. utayaṇaṉum taṉ cūḻcci veṟṟiyuṟum eṉṟa nampikkaiyil ataṉaip poṟuttuk koṇṭāṉ. utayaṇaṉ meḷaṉamāyirukkavē nakaikaḷaip putaitta iṭattaik kāṭṭumāṟu mīṇṭum avaṉait tūṇṭiṉāṉ vēṭar talaivaṉ. utayaṇaṉ talainimirntu avaṉaip pārttāṉ. “nīṅkaḷ iṭṭa neruppiṉ naṭuvē iruntatāl mikavum varuntiyuḷḷōm. uṭalmuḻutum kāntukiṟatu. nāṅkaḷ muṉpu taṅkiyirunta poḻilil ētō oru pakutiyilē poruḷkaḷaip putaittōm. tīyāl citaivupaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟa iṅkē, ippōtu ap pakuti etu eṉṟu kuṟippākat teriyavillai. ituvum uṅkaḷ tīyiṉāl vanta viṉaitāṉ. eṉavē anta neruppiṉ vēkam caṟṟut taṇintu āṟaṭṭum. āṟiyapiṉ nāṅkaḷ putaitta iṭattaik kāṭṭukiṟēṉ. atuvarai poṟuttiruṅkaḷ” eṉṟu taṉṉaik kūrntu nōkkum vēṭar talaivaṉiṉ, kaḻukuk kaṇkaḷai ūṭuruviyavāṟē talainimirntu maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭa vēṭar talaivaṉ “aḻal āṟiyapiṉ poruḷ putaitta iṭattai nī kāṭṭavillaiyāyiṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa uṉ karaṅkaḷ veṭṭappeṟuvatu uṟuti” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉiṭam kaṭumaiyāka moḻintāṉ.

vēṭar talaivaṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiyataik kēṭṭa tattai, tīyil viḻunta iḷantaḷireṉa maṇavāṭṭaṅ koṇṭu urukiṉāḷ. utayaṇaṉ tuyarcūḻa niṟkiṟāṉē eṉṟa niṉaivil kaṇṇirait tuḷittaṉa avaḷ kayal viḻikaḷ. kāñcaṉai, tattaiyait taḻuvik koṇṭavāṟē, “utayaṇaṉ tuyarai niṉ tuyarāka eṇṇupavaḷ nī! avaṉuṭaiya uyirkku ūṟu variṉ nī iṟattal orutalai. tantaiyaiyum avaṉ peruñ celvattaiyum nīkkik kātalaṉaip piṉpaṟṟi vanta uṉṉai viti ippaṭittāṉ naṭattum pōlum” eṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟiṉāḷ. ivarkaḷ ivvāṟu tuyaril aḻuntuvataik kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ ivarkaḷukku āṟutal kūṟa virumpiṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ vēṭar talaivaṉaiyum maṟṟavarkaḷaiyum nōkki, “uṅkaḷ viruppattukku māṟākāmal varuttantavirntu yāṅkaḷ putaitta poruḷkaḷaik kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉāl ippōtaikku eṉ kaikkaṭṭai aviḻttu viṭuṅkaḷ. piṉpu vēṇṭumāṉāl mīṇṭum kaṭṭik koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atil eṉakku maṟuppillai. ivaḷuṭaiya tuyarattait tēṟṟiya piṉ, tīppukai āṟiyatum poruḷkaḷaip putaitta iṭattaik kāṭṭuvēṉ” eṉṟaṉaṉ. ataik kēṭṭa vēṭarkaḷ mutalil maṟuppatupōṉṟu kaṭumaiyāka naṭantu koṇṭālum iṟutiyil ‘ivaṉ nam kaiyiṟ ciṟaip paṭṭavaṉ. poy collit tappa eṇṇi ivvāṟu kūṟiyiruppāṉāyiṉ ivaṉai uyirōṭu viṭamāṭṭōm. eṉavē ippōtaikku ivaṉ colvatai yellām ceytutāṉ vaippōmē’ eṉṟu karutik kaikkaṭṭai aviḻttuviṭṭaṉar. valaiyiliruntu viṭupaṭṭup piṇaiyai nōkki oṭum kalaimāṉaip pōlat tattaiyai nōkkic ceṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





20. paṭai vantatu!

araṇmaṉaip pañcaṇaikaḷil pūvaṉaiya mañcaṅkaḷilē tuyiṉṟa vācavatattai, aṉṟu aṅkē antap pālai maṇalilē taḷarntu cōrnta vaṇṇam kāñcaṉaiyiṉ maṭiyil paṭuttiruntatu utayaṇaṉai eṉṉavō ceytatu. vēṭarkaḷiṭamiruntu utayaṇaṉ kaikkaṭṭai aviḻttuk koṇṭu taṉṉai nōkki varuvatukaṇṭa tattai eḻuntāḷ. kāñcaṉai vilaki niṉṟu koṇṭāḷ. tīp pukaiyiṉālum naṭanta iḷaippiṉālum vāṭiyirunta tattaiyiṉ mukattil nāṇaṅ kalanta malarcci piṟantatu. avaḷait taṉ nīṇṭa kaikaḷāl mella taḻuvikkoṇṭē āṟutal kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. kuvaḷai malarkaḷiliruntu muttutirvatu pōlat tattaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷiliruntu corinta nīr muttukkaḷ, utayaṇaṉait tīṇṭiyatāl ēṟpaṭṭa makiḻcciyiṉ viḷaivākat tāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. eppōtum mikunta tuṉpattiṟku iṭaiyil tōṉṟum miṉṉal nēra makiḻcciyiltāṉ iṉpam mikuti. kaikkeṭṭukiṉṟa mallikaippūvaik kāṭṭilum naccup pāmpukaḷ cūḻntu vacikkum iṭattiluḷḷa maṉōrañcita malarukku maṇam atikamallavā? iṅkē ivarkaḷ nilai ivvāṟirukkap puṭpaka nakaram ceṉṟa vayantakaṉ nilaiyai aṟiya avaṉaip piṉpaṟṟuvōm.

‘āruyir naṇpaṉum aracakumāraṉumākiya utayaṇaṉait tuṉpattiṟkuriya cūḻnilaiyil taṉittirukkac ceytuviṭṭu vantirukkiṟōmē' eṉṟa kavalai orupuṟam. etirē maikkuḻampeṉak kuvintukiṭanta iruḷil puṭpaka nakarattiṉ pātai teriyātu pōyiṉa tuyaram orupuṟam. viraivil iṭavakaṉaic cantittup paṭaiyuṭaṉ tirumpa vēṇṭumeṉṟa paraparappu orupuṟam. ivvaḷavum cēra iruḷaik kiḻittu oṭum miṉṉalpōl muṉṉēṟic ceṉṟāṉ vayantakaṉ. etirē puṭpaka nakarattiṉ māṭamāḷikaikaḷil oḷi ceyta viḷakkukaḷum piṟavum taṉṉuṭaiya kaṇṇukkut terikiṉṟa aḷavu nakarai neruṅkiya piṟakutāṉ vayantakaṉukku uyir vantatu. appōtutāṉ pūtta mallikaipōlak kīḻ vāṉilum karumai nīṅki veḷittu utayamākak koṇṭiruntatu. vayantakaṉ puṭpaka nakaruḷ nuḻaintu araṇmaṉaiyiṉ koṭimatiṟ puṟattai aṭaintāṉ. kāvalar aṉumati peṟṟu iṭavakaṉaik kāṇa uḷḷē ceṉṟa vayantakaṉ. oṟṟaṉ oruvaṉiṭam ētō taṉittup pēcik koṇṭirunta nilaiyil aṅkē iṭavakaṉaik kaṇṭāṉ. vayantakaṉaik kaṇṭa iṭavakaṉ, oṟṟaṉai appaṭiyē viṭṭu viṭṭu āvalōṭu muṉvantu varavēṟṟāṉ. taṉṉai varavēṟṟa iṭavakaṉ mukattil tōṉṟiya kalakkamum tuyaramum, etu kāraṇamāka viḷaintavai eṉṟu vayantakaṉukkup pulappaṭa villai. utayaṇaṉuṭaiya nalattai vicāritta iṭavakaṉ mukattil paraparapput tōṉṟiyatum, utayaṇaṉ nalameṉpatai aṟintavuṭaṉ kalakalaveṉṟu vāyviṭṭuc cirittatum vayantakaṉukkup putirākavē iruntaṉa. antap putirai iṭavakaṉ aviḻttapōtu vayantakaṉum avaṉōṭu cērntu cirikka nērntatu. ‘utayaṇaṉuṭaiya ūkkam, uyarcci, oḻukkam, kalainalam ivaikaḷai ellām kaṇṭu poṟāmai koṇṭa piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, uḷḷē īṭṭi, vēl mutaliya āyutaṅkaḷaik kuttiṭṭu niṟuttiya poynnilam oṉṟai amaittu, vañcakamāka atilē vīḻtti utayaṇaṉaik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu kāṭṭu vēṭar mūlamākat tāṉ kēḷviyuṟṟatākavum ac ceytiyē taṉ tuyarattukkum paraparappiṟkum kāraṇameṉṟum iṭavakaṉ coṉṉapōtu, vayantakaṉ vāyviṭṭuc cirittāṉ. poy collukiṟavarkaḷ evvaḷavu aḻakāka ataṟkuk kai kālkaḷai oṭṭa vaittu uṇmaiyaippōla uruvākki viṭukiṟārkaḷ eṉpatai niṉaikkumpōtu vayantakaṉukku viyappāyiruntatu. taṉatu kāriyattiṉ turitattai niṉaiviṟ koṇṭu vantavaṉāy, utayaṇaṉuṭaiya appōtaiya nilaiyai avantiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭatu toṭaṅki ilavam putaril taṅkiyiruppatuvarai virivākak kūṟi, uṭaṉē paṭaiyutavi tēvaippaṭuvataiyum viḷakkiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. atōṭu taṉṉai nampuvataṟkāka utayaṇaṉ kūṟiya aṭaiyāḷa moḻiyaiyum vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉiṭam aṟivittāṉ. iṭavakaṉ uṭaṉē taṉ paṭaikaḷaip puṟappaṭumāṟu āṇaiyiṭṭāṉ. paṭaikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. iṭavakaṉum vayantakaṉum muṉceṉṟaṉar. paṭai piṉ toṭarntatu, iṭavakaṉ āṇaippaṭi paṭaikaḷukkuppiṉ vācavatattaikkum utayaṇaṉukkum uriyavākap palavakai alaṅkārap poruḷkaḷ cumantu koṇṭu varappeṟṟaṉa. aṇikalaṉkaḷum parivāramum paṇip peṇkaḷumāka ak kūṭṭam paṭaikkuppiṉ amaitiyākac ceṉṟatu.

paṭai kāṭṭuḷḷē vantuviṭṭatu. muṉ ceṉṟa vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum ilavamputarai aṭaintaṉar. aṅkē avarkaḷukkup periya ēmāṟṟam kāttiruntatu. putar eriyum putaiyumākat tīppaṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu putarukku etirē utayaṇiṉ ampukku ilakkākiya vēṭarkaḷ cintiya kurutiyum niṉamum parantiruntaṉa. mēlē anta niṇaviruntai etirnōkkik kaḻukukaḷum kākkaikaḷum vaṭṭa miṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉa. erintu koṇṭirunta ilavam putarum etirē terinta kurutip parappum kaṇṭa avarkaḷ utayaṇaṉukkum kāṭṭiluḷḷa vēṭarkaḷukkum ētō pōr naṭantirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu uyttuṇarntaṉar. āṉāl utayaṇaṉum tattai, kāñcaṉai ākiyōrkaḷum taṅkiyirunta ilavam putar tīppaṭṭu erintu koṇṭiruntatutāṉ vayantakaṉ aiyappaṭak kāraṇamākiyatu. ‘oruvēḷai utayaṇaṉ tattai mutaliyavarkaḷ neruppil aḻintu pōyirukkak kūṭumō?’ eṉpataṟkumēl vayantakaṉāl niṉaikkavē muṭiyavillai. avaṉ vāyviṭṭu aḻutē viṭṭāṉ. mēlē eṉṉa ceyvateṉṟu aṟiyāmal avarkaḷ aṅkēyē ayarntupōy amarntu viṭṭaṉar. utayaṇaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷukku ētēṉum tuyarnērntiruntāl tāṅkaḷum naṭpai nilaināṭṭa uyir viṭuvatākavē muṭivu ceytaṉar. iṭavakaṉum vayantakaṉum. itaṟkuḷ piṉ toṭarnta paṭaiyum aṅkē vantu cērntatu. peruntuyaruṭaṉ amarntirunta taṅkaḷ aracaṉaiyum vayantakaṉaiyum kaṇṇuṟṟa paṭait talaivarkaḷ varuntat takka ētō oṉṟu nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṉumāṉittuk koṇṭaṉar. vayantakaṉ vāyilāka naṭantavaṟṟai aṟintapiṉ avarkaḷ “tāṅkaḷ karutuvatupōla utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōrukkut tuyaram etuvum nērntirakkātu. itō puṟparappiṉ iṭaiyē teriyum aṭiccuvaṭukaḷ vēṭarkaḷuṭaiyaṉa. ic cuvaṭikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟāl utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōraic cantittālum cantikkalām” eṉṟu uṟutikūṟi, iṭavakaṉukkut tairiya mūṭṭiṉar. paṭait talaivar kūṟṟil caṟṟē nampikkai varappeṟṟavarāy vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum eḻuntu anta aṭiccuvaṭukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē piṉpaṟṟi naṭantaṉar. ēṉaiyōrum piṉ toṭarntaṉar. caṟṟut tolaivu ceṉṟatum paṭaittalaivaṉ malaic cariviṉ kīḻē ciṟuciṟu uruvilē eṟumpukkūṭṭam pōlat tōṉṟiya vēṭar kūṭṭam pulveḷiyil naṭuvē terintatu. antak kūṭṭattaip paṭaiyuṭaṉ neruṅkiya avarkaḷ, vēṭarkaḷukku iṭaiyē utayaṇaṉ, tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷuṭaṉ niṟpataik kaṇṭu aḷavilā makiḻcci aṭaintaṉar. -

iṭavakaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷ vēṭarai vaḷaittuk koṇṭaṉa. cuṟṟik karuṅkuvaḷai malar pūtta poykaiyuḷ naṭu maiyattil orē oru tāmarai pūttāṟ pōla niṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. cuṟṟi vaḷaittut tuṉpuṟuttik koṇṭirunta vēṭarkaḷ atircci aṭaintaṉar. pakkattil karuṅkāli marattiṉ mēlirunta paṟavaiyoṉṟu orumuṟai kattiyatu. ataik kēṭṭa nimittikaṉ, “iṉi namakkut tuṉpam nērum. nām ivviṭattai viṭṭu ōṭiviṭutal nallatu” eṉṟu vēṭarkaḷai nōkkik kūṟiṉāṉ. avarkaḷ yāvarum avaṉai veṟuppuṭaṉ erittuviṭuvatu pōlap pārttaṉar. nāṟpuṟamum citaṟi ōṭuvataṟkut toṭaṅkiya vēṭarkaḷ. iṟuti muṟaiyāka utayaṇaṉai neruṅkit tuṉpuṟuttalāyiṉar. ataik kaṇṭa iṭavakaṉ paṭaiyiṉar, viraivil vantu vēṭarkaḷai villum vāḷum vēlum koṇṭu etirttaṉar. itu utayaṇaṉakku ēṟpaṭuttiya nilai, irutalaik koḷḷi pōla iruntatu. ip puṟam vēṭarkaḷ tuyaram poṟukka muṭiyavillai. vantirukkum paṭaiyiṉar taṉakku vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu terintuviṭṭāl, pōkiṉṟa pōkkil ciṉantīra ētāvatu ceytuviṭa vēṭarkaḷ tayaṅka māṭṭārkaḷ. itaṟkāka vantiruppavar evareṉpataiyē aṟintu koḷḷātavaṉpōla utayaṇaṉ naṭikka nērntatu. “vantirukkiṉṟa paṭaiyiṉar uṅkaḷaic cērntavarkaḷā? piṟarā? ivarkaḷāl namakku ētēṉum tuṉpam nērumāyiṉ eṅkaḷai iṅkē eṅkāvatu maṟaintirukkac ceyyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟu vēṭarkaḷai nōkkik kūṟi ēmāṟṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

ataṟku vēṭarkaḷ “itu utayaṇaṉiṉ mantirikaḷākiya iṭavakaṉ paṭai. uyirtappa virumpiṉāyāyiṉ eṅkaḷōṭu ōṭivaruka” eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟiviṭṭut tāṅkaḷ tappa vaḻitēṭi, virumpiya ticaikaḷil oṭalāyiṉar. cilar puṟputarkaḷil oḷintu ōṭiṉar. patukkaik kaṟkaḷiṉ iṭaiyilē patuṅkiyavāṟu viraintaṉar vēṟu cilar. eñciyavarkaḷil iṭavakaṉ paṭaivīraruṭaiya vāḷukku iraiyāyiṉar cilar. paṭaivīrar viyakka, vēṭṭuvap pōrttiṟaṅ kāṭṭip pōriṭṭaṉar cilar. vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum kūṭap pōril īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar pōlum. vēṭṭuvarkaḷil perumpālōr ōṭi viṭṭārkaḷēṉum, eñciyavarāy niṉṟu pōriṭṭa cilarukkē iṭavakaṉ paṭai vīrarkaḷ muṟṟilum muyaṉṟu viṭaikūṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. in nilaiyil utayaṇaṉ, tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷuṭaṉē ciṟitu vilaki otukkup puṟamāka ōriṭattil maṟaintirukka virumpiṉāṉ. ataṟku vēṟōr kāraṇamum iruntatu. tāṉum tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷum irunta iṭam iraṇṭu tarattup paṭaiyiṉarukkum iṭaiyil amaintiruntatu. ‘avarkaḷukkuḷ parimāṟik koḷḷum ampukaḷ vēlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭaiyilirukkum taṅkaḷukku ētēṉum ūṟu ceytalum kūṭum’ eṉṟu karutip pakkattil maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu vaḷarntirunta oriṭattil tattai, kāñcaṉai ivarkaḷuṭaṉ maṟaivākac ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉ iruntu koṇṭāṉ.

pōr oru vaḻiyāka muṭintatu. cīṟi eḻunta pāmpiṉ muṉ elikkaṇampōla maṟainta cuvaṭu teriyātapaṭi āki viṭṭatu vēṭar paṭai. utayaṇaṉ veḷiyē vantāṉ. vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ mutaliyōrum paṭait talaivarkaḷum aḷappariya maṉamakiḻcciyuṭaṉ tiṅkaḷaic cūḻnta viṇ mīṉiṉampōla utayaṇaṉaic cūḻntukoṇṭu veṟṟik kaḷippu viḷaṅka āravāram ceytaṉar. pirinta naṇparkaḷ kūṭiṉar. pēcariya maṉaniṟaivai aṭaintaṉar. iṭavakaṉ, utayaṇaṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭu kaṇṇirviṭṭa kāṭci, kūṭiyiruntavarkaḷai urukkiyatu. allal akaṉṟa makiḻcciyil tattaiyum kāñcaṉaiyum veḷivantu orupuṟamāka niṉṟu koṇṭaṉar. aṭukkaṭukkāka eḻuntu vanta tuṉpaṅkaḷai ariya tuṇaiyāka niṉṟu pōkkiya vayantakaṉ ippōtu iṭavakaṉ tuṇaiyuṭaṉ takka taruṇattil vantu utaviyirā viṭṭāl taṉkati eṉṉa ākum?’ eṉṟu ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉ maṉa eṇṇaṅkaḷ caṭṭeṉṟu taṭaippaṭṭaṉa. etirē vayantakaṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. appōtu avaṉai utayaṇaṉ pārtta kaṉinta pārvaiyil naṉṟiyaṟivu pūraṇamākak kaṉintu terintatu.

appōtaikkup pakkattilirunta ciṟu cōlai oṉṟil yāvarum taṅkiṉar. veku nāḷkaḷukkup piṟaku mīṇṭum cantikkiṉṟārkaḷākaiyāl iṭavakaṉum utayaṇaṉum iṭai viṭātu pēcikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. vayantakaṉ pakkattil amarntiruntāṉ. tuṉpap peruṅkaṭalaik kaṭakka iṉpap puṇaiyāka vantutaviṉarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷiṭam mīṇṭum nā taḻutaḻukka naṉṟi kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





﻿21. malaiccāralilē

cōlaiyil taṅki utayaṇaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ aḷavaḷāvik koṇṭiruntapōtu iṭavakaṉiṉ paṭaivīrarkaḷ pakkattilē irunta cayanti nakarattaic cārnta malaiccāralil, utayaṇaṉ tattai mutaliyōr taṅkuvataṟkēṟṟa paṭa māṭaṅkaḷaiyum ēṉaiyōrkkuriya paṭaivīṭukaḷaiyum amaittuk koṇṭiruntaṉar. maṟukum muṟṟamum muṉṟilumāka vakuttu amaikkappaṭṭirunta antap pāṭi vīṭukaḷiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷ malaiccāralil taṉi aḻakuṭaṉē tōṉṟiṉa. tūyaveṇ paṭṭukkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa paṭa māṭaṅkaḷ tolaiviliruntu kāṇpataṟku vicumpilē kaṭṭiya kaṇṇāṭi nakar pōlat terintaṉa. vācavatattaikku iyaṟṟiya pāṭi vīṭṭil paḷḷimāṭam, aṉṟilum viḷaiyāṭu muṉṟil mutaliyavaṟṟai aḻakuṟac camaittiruntaṉar. cantaṉap palakai, maṇikkalap pēḻai irukkaik kaṭṭil, ālavaṭṭam mutaliya alaṅkārap poruḷ atil nirampiyiruntaṉa. utayaṇaṉukkuriya paṭamāṭak kōvililum oru ciṟanta aracaṉukku vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum poruḷkaḷum mikuntirukkumāṟu iyaṟṟappaṭṭiruntatu. palavakaik kuṟṟiḷaiyōr āṭavarum makaḷirumākap paṇi ceyyak kāttiruntaṉar. oruvarumē aṟṟa cūṉiyappiratēcamākiya antak kāṭṭil, malaiccāralil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nakaril ivvaḷavu pēr tōṉṟiyatu puṟṟiliruntu īcal puṟappaṭṭatu pōliruntatu. nalla nēram pārttu utayaṇaṉ mutaliya yāvarum cōlai nīṅki malaiccāralil amaikkap peṟṟa nakaril kuṭipukuntaṉar. peṇyāṉaiyiṉmītu vēkamāka vanta uṭal nōvutīra maruttuva muṟaippaṭi uṇavu camaikkap paṭṭatu. nāḻikaik kaṇakkar uṇṇa vēṇṭiya poḻutaṟintu kūṟiṉar. iravum pakalumāka viṭātu payaṇam ceyta aluppu utayaṇaṉaic cōrvu koḷḷac ceytiruntatu. vēṭarkaḷōṭu pōr ceyta kaḷaippu vēṟu avaṉai varuttiyatu. uṭal aluppu nīṅka eṇṇey pūci nīrāṭiviṭṭup piṉpu uṇṇalām eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. uṭal oḷikuṟaintu vāṭṭaṅ kaṇṭiruntatu. vallavaṉ vakutta vācaṉai poruntiya eṇṇeyaip pūcikkoṇṭu kuḷirnta nīril maṉam virumpi veku nēram uṭal kuḷira āṭiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. nīrāṭi muṭintapiṉ naṇparkaḷ oṉṟāka uṇṇa amarntaṉar. veku nāḷkaḷukkup piṟaku nērnta inta ariya amaitiyāṉa vāyppu mūvar maṉattilum makiḻcciyai niṟaiyac ceytiruntatu. makiḻcci niṟaivōṭu utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ mūvarum uṇṭaṉar. cirippum viḷaiyāṭṭumāka uṇṭu muṭikka nēramāyviṭṭālum uvakai mikutiyil poḻutu pōṉatē teriyavillai naṇparkaḷukku. piṭiyil vaḻippayaṇam ceyta iḷaippu. uṟakkamiṉṟip pala iravukaḷaik kaḻittamai, vēṭarkaḷ tollai, tīyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa vētaṉai, ivvaḷavum tīra naṉṟāka ōyvu eṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

vācavatattaiyai āyamakaḷir naṉku nīrāṭṭiṉar. aḻakiya kaṟcuṉaiyiṉ naṭuvē poṟpāvaipōla niṉṟu, kāṇpōr makiḻa naṟunīrāṭiṉāḷ tattai. nīrāṭiyapiṉ pacitīra amutam pōṉṟa uṇavai uṇṇac ceytaṉar. iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷāka uṇṇāmaliruntu paci mikuntirunta tattai naṉku uṇṭāḷ. cuvaimikkatākac camaikkap paṭṭiruntatu anta nalluṇavu. uṇavu muṭintapiṉ tōḻip peṇkaḷ tuyartīrap pala paṇiviṭaikaḷ ceytaṉar. yāvarum oyvu koṇṭaṉar. appōtu malaiccāralil nakariṉ amaitiyāṉa tōṟṟam aḻakiṉ ellaiyāka viḷaṅkiyatu. paṭaiyōṭu vantirunta kutiraikaḷ, yāṉaikaḷ ivai yāvum periya periya maraṅkaḷilē kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. nakariṉ amaitiyaik kalaittuk koṇṭu yāṉaikaḷiṉ muḻakkam malaiccāralil kēṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu. pōkapūmiyaik kāṭṭilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya antac ceyaṟkai nakariṉ nūlveṇ māṭaṅkaḷ pāṟkaṭaliṉ alaikaḷaip pōlak kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. moyttuk kiṭanta paṭai vīrarkaḷ, kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka oyvupeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.

utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōr iḷaippāṟi eḻuntatum pakkattiluḷḷa cayanti nakarattiṟkuc celluvataṟkut tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar. mērumalai mēl eḻunta ceṅkatirc celvaṉ pōla aḻakiṟ ciṟanta yāṉai oṉṟiṉmītu ēṟi amarntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉum iṭivakaṉum pakkattē vēṟu yāṉaikaḷiṉmītu ēṟi varalāyiṉar. irupuṟamum yāṉaikaḷ cūḻa naṭuvil utayaṇaṉ irunta tōṟṟam, cuṟṟilum karu mukil iruppa naṭuvē cuṭar iruntateṉa viḷaṅkiṟṟu. avaṉ talaikkumēlē niḻal ceytu koṇṭirunta, veṇkoṟṟakkuṭai, taṇmati pōlat terintatu.

nirai niraiyāka villum vāḷum vēlum kēṭayamum ēntip paṭaikaḷ piṉceṉṟaṉa. yāṉai puravi tēr mutaliyaṉa varicaiyākap piṉpaṟṟip pōyiṉa. potiyiṟ cantaṉamum vintiyattu yāṉaik kompukaḷum mērumalaip poṉṉum kuṭakaṭaliṟ piṟanta paṭarkoṭip pavaḻamum teṉkaṭaṟ piṟanta miṉṉoḷi muttum īḻattuc ceppum imayattu vayiramum ākiya alaṅkārap poruḷ pala ēntiya ēvaliḷaiyar avarkaḷukkup piṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. yavaṉattut taccarum avanti nāṭṭuk kollarum makatanāṭṭu maṇiviṉaiñarum pāṭali nāṭṭup poṟṟoḻilāḷarum kōcalattu oviyarum vattavanāṭṭu vaṇṇakkammarum ākiya toḻiṟ celvarkaḷiṉ kalaik kūṭṭam appāl pōyiṟṟu. kāṭṭil utayaṇaṉukku utavavē iṭavakaṉ vantāṉ eṉiṉum, taṉakkup pēraracaṉākiya utayaṇaṉai avaṉ amaiccaruḷ oruvaṉākiya tāṉ kāṇac cellumpōtu takka mariyātaikaḷuṭaṉ cella vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum utayaṇaṉai maṇañ ceytukoḷḷa avaṉ āruyirk kātali tattaiyum uṭaṉ vantiruppatāl ēṟṟa alaṅkārap poruḷkaḷuṭaṉum etir koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiyē ivvāṟu pal poruḷum pala viṉaiñarum koṇṭu vantiruntāṉ iṭavakaṉ. vācavatattai kollappaṇṭi (oruvakai cittirac civikai) oṉṟil kāñcaṉai tuṇai niṉṟu ēṟṟa ēṟikkoṇṭāḷ. kūṭiyirunta makkaḷ kūṭṭattai vilakkak kaikkōṟ cilatar kuraliṭṭa olikaḷukkiṭaiyē puṟap pāṭṭukku aṟikuṟiyāṉa muraca oliyum kalantolittatu.

malaiccāraliluḷḷa ciṟṟurkaḷil vāḻum kuṟunila makkaḷākiya kuṟumparkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷum muṉṉarē iṭavakaṉ mūlam utayaṇaṉuṭaiya varavu kuṟittu olaic ceyti peṟṟiruntaṉar. aṅkaṅkē kūṭi niṉṟu utayaṇaṉuṭaiya aṭaiyāḷa ilacciṉaiyai mariyātaiyuṭaṉ kaṇṭuṇarntu palvakait tiṟaip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ cantittaṉar. kuṟumpar makkaḷ, avarkaḷ talaivar mutaliyōr, yāṉait tattamum malaittēṉum pala kaṉivakaikaḷum vīṇai ceyvataṟkēṟṟa marat taṇṭukaḷum mūṅkilil viṉainta muttukkaḷum akilum pulittōlum ākiya pala poruḷkaḷai aṉpuṭaṉ kaiyuṟaiyākak koṭuttaṉar. ‘ekkālattum niṉ kuṭiyiṉarāka vāḻum pēṟu emakkuk kiṭṭuka’ eṉṟa avarkaḷ vēṇṭukōḷai utayaṇaṉ makiḻcciyōṭu varavēṟṟu mikka naṉṟi celuttiyapiṉ, avarkaḷiṭam viṭaipeṟṟu mēṟceṉṟāṉ. appāl ivvāṟē pala ciṟuciṟu malaikaḷaiyum kāṭukaḷaiyum ciṟṟuārkaḷaiyum kaṭantu payaṇattait toṭarntaṉar. iṭaiiṭaiyē taṉ kīḻ vāḻum kuṭi makkaḷiṉ aṉpu kaṉinta varavēṟpu utayaṇaṉukku maṉakiḻvai aḷittatu. nikaḻnta tuṉpaṅkaḷai maṟappataṟku itu nalla vāyppākavum amaintatu. malaip pakutikaḷilum kāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻum am makkaḷ kaḷḷaṅkapaṭam aṟṟavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṉpu vañcaṉai kalavāta tūymai vāyntatu. atil taṅkaḷ aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil matippum kalantiruntatu. poḻutu cāyum nēram. yāvarum cayanti nakarattai neruṅkiṉar. aḻakiṟ ciṟanta cayanti nakaram mālai nērattu mañcaḷ veyilil poṉ mayamākak kāṭci aḷittatu. tēvakōṭṭattu maṇiyōcai varuvōrellām varuka eṉṟu aḻaippatupōl olittuk koṇṭiruntatu. cayanti nakarai utayaṇaṉuṭaiya amaiccarkaḷil oruvaṉākiya urumaṇṇuvā āṇṭu vantāṉ. cayanti nakaram utayaṇaṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭatāyiṉ, avaṉ āṇaiyiṉmēl urumaṇṇuvā āṇṭu vantāṉ. cayanti nāṟpuṟamum iyaṟkai vaḷamikka malaikaḷ cūḻnta nakar. eṉṟum kuṉṟāta eḻilvaḷamum cārntatu.

anta nakarattiṟkuḷ tattai, kāñcaṉai, vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ ivarkaḷ puṭaicūḻa mikka viruppattuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉ pukuntaṉaṉ. paṭaikaḷ yāvum puṟanakarilē taṅki viṭṭaṉa. utayaṇaṉ varavu muṉpē olaic ceyti mūlam aṟivikkappaṭṭiruntataṉāl, nakaril eṅkum kaḷippuṭaṉ kūṭiya āravāram mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭatu. pūraṇa viḷakku, tōraṇa mālaikaḷum cuṭaroḷi vitāṉaṅkaḷumākap polivum putumaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa, nakarapperu vītikaḷ yāvum. avaṟṟai makiḻcciyuṭaṉ nōkkiyavāṟē ivarkaḷ ceṉṟaṉar.





22. cayanti nakaril tirumaṇam

katiravaṉuṭaiya vemmaiyai āṟṟic cayanti nakarukku niḻalaḷippavaṉpōla viritta pūṅkoṭikaḷ pala tōṉṟiṉa. periya muracaṅkaḷiṉ kaṭal pōṉṟa olikku iṭaiyē, kuṭamuḻākkaḷiṉ kiṇkiṇi icaiyum olittatu. iruviṉaip paḻantuyarkaḷai veṉṟa arukatēvaṉukku intira ulakam varavēṟpaḷittatu pōla, utayaṇaṉ tattai mutaliyōrai makaḷirum maintarumākak kūṭic cayanti nakarattiṉar maṅkala niṟai kuṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭu varavēṟṟaṉar. iruḷ kaṭintu tōṉṟum iḷañāyiṟu pōlat tuyar kaṭintu poliyum tōṉṟalē varuka eṉṟār cilar. cayanti nakariṉar tam aṉpu niṟaivai ellām utayaṇaṉ varavēṟpil kāṭṭiṉar. “yāṉaiyai mīṇṭum peṟak kāṉiṟ ceṉṟapōtu pakai maṉṉaṉāl paṟṟik koḷḷappaṭṭāy! ippōtu veṟṟiyaiyum ataṉ ciṉṉamāka vācavatattaiyaiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu vantirukkiṟāy! uṉakkup putalvar pōṉṟa uṉ kuṭikaḷai iṉit tuṉpam nīṅka āḷavēṇṭiyatu uṉ kaṭamai!” eṉṟu mutumakkaḷ vāḻttiṉar. aracaṉ, kuṭikaḷ eṉṟa uyarvu tāḻvu aṉpu vāḻttukku ētu? “tirumāl mārpil tirumakaḷpōl eṅkaḷ maṉṉaṉ neñcil iṭai viṭātu uṟaiyum pēṟu uṉakku eytuka! nī varakkāṇa yāṅkaḷ muṟpiṟaviyil nalviṉai mikutiyākac ceytiruntōm pōlum. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya makaḷ mikac ciṟanta aḻakiyeṉak kēḷviyuṟṟatuṇṭu. anta aḻakai nērilēyē kaṇkuḷirak kāṇac ceyta em aracaṉ vāḻka!” eṉṟu tattaiyaip palar vāḻttiṉar. anta vāḻttil ‘avaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭaracaṉukku viraivil kōpperuntēvi eṉṟa matippukkuriyavaḷ āvāḷ’ eṉṉum perumaiyum kalantiruntatu. vēṟu cilar, ivvaḷavu naṟceyalkaḷum piḻai paṭāmal nikaḻvataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātavaṉāy iruntavaṉ yūki eṉpataik kēḷviyuṟṟu, avaṉai vāyāṟa vāḻttiṉar. “naṉṟāka vanta oru poruḷ, ulakil oruvarkkut tītāy muṭintālum, viyappataṟku illai. tītāy vanta oru poruḷ. naṉṟāy muṭintu nalam payattalum, attakaiyatu tāṉ. māya yāṉaiyāl nam maṉṉaṉai vañcakamākac ciṟai ceyta piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya tīmai, nam maṉṉaṉukku nalamāy muṭintatōṭu allāmal, aḻakiṟ ciṟanta naṅkai tattaiyaiyum aḷittirukkiṟatu. makaḷaik koṭuppōr mutalil tītu pōṟ kāṭṭip piṉpu ceyyumnalam ivvāṟu kuṟippākavē irukkumō?”

ippaṭip palappala eṇṇiṉar cayanti nakarak kuṭi makkaḷ. ittakaiya palvakaiyāṉa āravāraṅkaḷukkum iṭaiyē, amarāpatiyiṟ pukum intiraṉaippōlat tattai mutaliyōr piṉtoṭara utayaṇaṉ araṇmaṉaiyuḷ pukuntāṉ. utayaṇaṉ cayanti nakarukku vantapiṉ cila nāḷkaḷ amaitiyākavum iṉpamākavum kaḻintaṉa. uṟavum cuṟṟamum oṉṟu kūṭiṭak kaḻinta anta nāḷkaḷ, maṟakka muṭiyātaṉa. annāḷkaḷiṭaiyē orunāḷ vācavatattaiyiṉ tirumaṇa niṉaivai am maṇattaic ceytu koḷvataṟku uriyavaṉāṉa utayaṇaṉukku naṇparkaḷ uṇṭākkiṉar. ataṉ payaṉāka araṇmaṉaik kaṇiyaṉ oru maṅkala nāḷai maṇattiṟku ēṟṟateṉṟu vakuttuk koṭuttāṉ. cōtiṭanūṟ poruḷum tarukka nūṟporuḷum naṉkuaṟintu tērntavaṉ antak kaṇi. araṇmaṉaiyil avaṉukkup palavakai mariyātaikaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ kūṟiya nāḷai nāṭaṟiya eṭutturaikkumāṟu vaḷḷuvarkaḷ āṇai peṟṟaṉar. vēṟṟu nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷukkut takka tūtuvarkaḷ mūlamākat tirumaṇa olaikaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. muracuk koṭṭiluḷ teyva vaḻi pāṭṭuṭaṉum arumaiyuṭaṉum pēṇappeṟṟu vanta periya periya muracaṅkaḷait tirunāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum paṭai nāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum maṇanāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum yāṉai mēlēṟṟi ūraṟiya olikkac ceytukoṇṭē ceyti kūṟuvatu vaḷḷuvar vaḻakkam. muracai nīrāṭṭic cantaṉam pūci mālaiyaṇintu yāṉai mēlēṟṟikkoṇṭu utayaṇaṉ tirumaṇa nāḷai aṟivippataṟku vaḷḷuvar pala ticaikaḷilum ceṉṟaṉar.

maṉṉaṉ tirumaṇac ceyti kēṭṭa kuṭimakkaḷ nakaraip palavakaiyālum alaṅkarikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. poṉṉilum muttilum maṇiyilum palvakait tōraṇaṅkaḷ ceytu peru vītikaḷ tōṟum kaṭṭiṉar. vīṭukaḷ, peru vāyil, vītit toṭakkaṅkaḷ, mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilellām vāḻai maramum pākku maramumākak kaṭṭi aḻaku ceytaṉar. nakariluḷḷa aṟac cālaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vantavarkaḷukkellām uṇavaḷikkumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉar. iḷanaṅkaiyarkaḷum ēval makaḷirum kuṅkumam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuḻaittu aḻakiya vēṟu pala vaṇṇak kuḻampukaḷaiyum koṇṭu terukkaḷil kavarcciyāṉa kōlaṅkaḷai iṭṭaṉar. kōvilkaḷilum kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟappāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷ nikaḻttap peṟṟaṉa. palavakaiyilum taṅkaḷ aracar tirumaṇa nikaḻcciyiṟ paṅku koṇṭavarpōla nakarai aḻakiṟkumēl aḻaku ceytu makiḻntaṉar makkaḷ. tirumaṇa naṉṉāḷ vantatu. nakareṅkum āravāramum makiḻcciyum niṟaintu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pala nāṭukaḷiṉ aracarum vantiruntaṉar. tirumaṇa nīrāṭṭuvataṟkāka aḻakiṟ ciṟanta kaṉṉi makaḷir mañcaṉanīr koṇṭuvarap puṟappaṭṭaṉar. cilampukaḷ kālil oli ceyyak koṭi iṭaiyil kuṭattunīr calacalakka mañcaṉanīr koṇṭu vantataṟkup piṟaku, avarkaḷ nalla nāḻikai nōkki nīrāṭṭak kāttiruntaṉar. nāḻikai nōkki irunta nāḻikaik kaṇakkar, ēṟṟa nēram vantatum kūṟiṉar. tī vēṭṭu maṇam nikaḻttavēṇṭiya poḻutum vantu cērntatu.

muṟaippaṭi amaikkappaṭṭirunta tirumaṇap pantaril naṭuvē maṟaivalāḷar mantiram ota, maṇa vēḷvi ceytāṉ utayaṇaṉ. pakkattilē tattai maṉakkōlattil eḻuti vaitta cittirap poṟpāvaipōla amarntiruntāḷ. kāmaṉum iratiyum aṭuttaṭuttu amarntiruntatu pōlak kāṇpōrkkut tōṉṟiyatu, ik kāṭci. iṭaiyē avaṉ tattaiyaik kaip piṭittu aruntati kāṭṭiya tōṟṟam, kāṇa makiḻcci aḷippatāy iruntatu. aruntati kāṭṭi, vaciṭṭaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu, nāṉmukak kaṭavuḷai vēḷvik kuḻiyil avaṉukkuriya ticaiyil vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. teyva nivētaṉaṅkaḷ ceytavāṟē maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷai aṭuttaṭuttu iyaṟṟiṉāṉ maṇamakaṉākiya avaṉ.

maṇamākāta kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ palar uḻuntu, nel, uppu, malar, veṟṟilaic curuḷ, cantaṉam mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷait tattaiyiṉ kaiyil aḷittu, ēḻu muṟai kaṇavaṉaik kaikūppi vaṇaṅkumpaṭi vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. kāñcaṉai, tattaikkup pakkattil iruntu, avaḷ nāṇam aṭaiyā vaṇṇam utayaṇaṉōṭu neruṅki uṭkārumāṟu ceytāḷ. avaḷuṭaiya melliya malarai otta aṭikaḷait taṉ kaiyāl paṟṟi ammimēl vaittapōtum, avaḷ pūṅkaraṅkaḷil pori cinti vēḷvikku iṭumāṟu ceytapōtum iṉpa uṇarcciyāl pullarippu ēṟpaṭṭatu utayaṇaṉukku, maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ ellām muṭintapiṉ tattaiyiṉ valakkaiyaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu tī valam vantāṉ utayaṇaṉ. mutiyōrkaḷ nallāci kūṟiṉar. avaḷaik kaip paṟṟi naṭantapōtu, mella aṭipeyarttu vaittatu, ‘tantai, tāy, uṟaviṉaraṟṟa anta iṭattil valiya taṉ kātalaṉai maṇam ceytu koṇṭatai eṇṇi, avaḷ taḷarcciyaṭaintu viṭṭāḷō?’ eṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkut tōṉṟiyatu. palavitac ciṟappukkaḷuṭaṉ eḻilmikkatāka alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirunta maṇavaṟaip paḷḷikkaṭṭilil ēṟumpōtu, iruvarukkumē entavitamāṉa taḷarvumillai. naṉṟāka alaṅkāram ceytu tattaiyai utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ aṉuppiya kāñcaṉai, itaḻk kaṭaiyil oru kuṟumpup puṉṉakaiyai neḷiyaviṭṭuk koṇṭē nakarntāḷ. talai kuṉintavāṟē utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ paḷḷiyaṟaiyai nōkki naṭantāḷ vācavatattai. itaṉ piṟaku āṟu tiṅkaḷ makiḻcciyākavum amaitiyākavum ceṉṟatu. urumaṇṇuvāviṉ poṟuppil viṭṭuc ceṉṟirunta cayanti nakara āṭci eppōtum pōlak kuṭikaḷ makiḻa āḷappaṭṭu vantatu. utayaṇaṉ aṅkiruntum, appōtutāṉ tirumaṇamākiyirunta avaṉukku āṭcip poṟuppukkaḷaic ceytaliṉāl ceyal mikuntu cōrvu tōṉṟum eṉṟu karuti, urumaṇṇuvāvē avvāṭcip poṟuppai mēṟpārttu vantāṉ. yāvum iṉitē nikaḻntu vantaṉa. tirumaṇamāṉa piṉpu āṟāvatu tiṅkaḷil ceyvataṟku uriya uṭaṉ mayir kaḷaital eṉṟōr caṭaṅkukku ēṟṟa nallanāḷ vantatu. ciṟappāka ac caṭaṅku nikaḻntu muṭintapiṉ, eṇṇey nīrāṭṭup peṟutal marapu. āyiratteṭṭup poṟkuṭaṅkaḷiṉ mañcaṉa nīr koṇṭuvarap peṟṟatu. utayaṇaṉukku āṭavarum tattaikku āyattup peṇṭirum eṇṇey aṇintaṉar. 'maṉaiyaṟam ciṟakkumāṟu kaṟpāl māṭci peṟuka’ eṉṟu vāḻttiyavāṟē āyamakaḷir neyyaṇintaṉar.
